Meanwhile, Specula addresses limitations of LLM-driven techniques like reward hacking and hallucinations through self-evolving loops that iteratively improve specification quality by enabling the agents to deepen their understanding of system code and its behaviors. We have used Specula to check 48 open-source system projects; Specula found 249 bugs including many deep bugs that are hard to find by existing approaches. Specula has been used by several companies and is maintained at https://github.com/specula-org/Specula.

</details>


### [146] [CodeNib: A Multi-View Data System for Serving Repository Context to Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25431)
*Zhongming Yu,Hengjia Yu,Boqin Yuan,Shuting Zhao,Yizhao Chen,Aryan Dokania,Mihir Jagtap,Jiayu Chang,Yitong Ma,Yash Jayswal,Wentao Ni,Hejia Zhang,Zhaoling Chen,Gangda Deng,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeNib通过构建可复用的仓库视图（词法、稠密、结构），实现跨提交的高效上下文检索，在更新速度、导航延迟和令牌节省方面均显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理在检索仓库上下文时，因索引、语言服务器和任务历史彼此隔离，导致反复发现和生命周期成本不透明的问题。

Method: 提出CodeNib系统，为每次仓库提交构建可复用的词法、稠密和结构视图，映射到仓库相对源码范围，跨编辑维护选定视图，并通过单一运行时提供排序搜索、符号导航和限定上下文服务。

Result: 在100个快照上，图更新和向量更新中位数分别比独立重建快8.7倍和25.4倍；静态导航请求中位延迟比为4.7倍；五种模型下上下文策略减少50%-87%的轨迹token。

Conclusion: CodeNib支持多视图仓库上下文服务，具备明确、操作特定的有效性边界，显著提升效率。

Abstract: Coding agents repeatedly search, navigate, and retain context from evolving repositories, but disconnected indexes, language servers, and task-local histories force repeated discovery and obscure lifecycle costs. CodeNib builds reusable lexical, dense, and structural views per repository commit, maps outputs to repository-relative source ranges, maintains selected views across edits, and serves ranked search, symbol navigation, and bounded context through one runtime.
  Across 100 snapshots, we map quality-cost frontiers across the repository-context lifecycle. When outputs match an independent rebuild, graph and vector updates are $8.7\times$ and $25.4\times$ faster at the median. On the static-navigation subset matching normalized live-server locations (63% of 1,000 requests), the median per-request live/static latency ratio is $4.7\times$. Across five models, selected context policies preserve localization with 50--87% fewer trajectory tokens than paired grep/read. Together, these results support multi-view repository-context serving with explicit, operation-specific validity boundaries.

</details>


### [147] [Repositories, Contributors, and Continuity: An Empirical Study of Foundational Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25437)
*Vincent Gierisch,Nicole Hoess,Ralf Ramsauer,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实证软件工程方法分析量子软件仓库的演化，发现贡献者迁移模式和跨项目知识转移的证据，揭示了概念在仓库边界间持续存在的多种发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态系统快速成长，但许多仓库的开发活动仅持续有限时间，引发了概念是否能在单个仓库之外持续存在的问题，需要实证研究来理解概念和影响力如何在仓库间传播。

Method: 应用成熟的实证软件工程技术，结合跨仓库活动分析与贡献者关系网络，研究一组基础量子软件仓库及其社区演化。

Result: 发现了贡献者迁移模式和跨项目知识转移的实证证据；观察到多种发展路径：项目可演化为持续社区、贡献者可将概念整合到已有生态系统中、或通过新的衍生软件产品继续活动。

Conclusion: 初步提供了关于概念和影响力如何在量子软件生态系统的仓库边界间持续存在的实证视角。

Abstract: Driven by contributions from academia, industry, and open-source communities, the quantum software ecosystem is rapidly growing. Across this ecosystem, new concepts often emerge through software artefacts accompanying scientific publications as well as through sustained development in larger communities. However, many repositories receive development efforts only over a limited period of time, raising the question whether their concepts persist beyond individual repositories. In this paper, we apply established empirical software engineering techniques to analyse a set of foundational quantum software repositories. We combine cross-repository activity with contributor relationships to study the evolution of communities. Our analysis provides empirical evidence of contributor migration patterns and indications of cross-project knowledge transfer. We observe multiple development paths: projects may evolve into sustained communities, contributors may integrate concepts into established ecosystems, or activity may continue through new and follow-up software artefacts. Our observations provide an initial empirical perspective on how concepts and influence persist across repository boundaries in quantum software ecosystems.

</details>


### [148] [Input Relation Prompting for Metamorphic Testing on Query-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25603)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Shin-Jie Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对查询系统缺乏测试预言的问题，提出一种辅助测试人员识别蜕变关系的自动化方法，通过输入-输出关系描述实现缺陷检测，案例研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询系统测试面临两大挑战：缺乏验证所需的真实标准（ground truth），以及手动测试耗时耗力。现有方法过度依赖领域知识，亟需更高效的自动化测试方案。

Method: 提出一种辅助识别蜕变关系（MRs）的自动化方法，通过提示描述输入-输出关系的MRs，帮助测试人员应用蜕变测试（MT）。该方法不依赖预定义测试用例或真实标准，可与其他测试方法（如组合测试、模糊测试）结合使用。

Result: 通过真实Web应用的案例研究，证明了该方法在实际查询系统中的适用性和潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法为蜕变测试领域提供了新工具，能够有效提升查询系统测试效率，弥补传统方法在缺乏真实标准场景下的不足。

Abstract: Testing query-based systems (QBSs) presents significant challenges due to the absence of ground truth for validation and the extensive time and effort required for manual testing. This paper addresses these challenges by proposing an approach that assists testers in identifying metamorphic relations (MRs) for metamorphic testing (MT) instead of solely and exhaustively relying on prerequisite domain knowledge. MT is an approach rising in popularity employed to alleviate the oracle problem by applying input transformation rules (MRs) to a program. The proposed approach helps the tester by prompting MRs that describe the relationships between inputs and outputs, enabling fault detection when the expected relationship is not met. Unlike traditional testing approaches, this approach does not rely on pre-defined test cases or concrete ground truth, making it suitable for the testing of real-world QBSs. Furthermore, the proposed approach can be combined with other testing methods such as combinatorial testing and fuzz testing, expanding the possibilities for QBS testing. A conducted case study of a real-world web application demonstrates the applicability and potential of the proposed approach. Overall, this research contributes to advancing the field of metamorphic testing and provides a valuable tool for QBS testers to enhance their testing efficiency.

</details>


### [149] [SkillGate: Cost Efficient Runtime Malicious Skill File Detection in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25619)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGate，一种针对AI编程Agent技能文件供应链攻击的安全网关。通过混合正则预过滤+LLM评判流水线，在SkillsBench基准上实现F1=0.817、FPR=1.13%，LLM输入token减少77%，性能超过现有工具5-6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程Agent（如Cursor、Claude Code、Copilot）广泛使用的技能（skill）文件缺乏安全审查，存在供应链攻击风险——恶意技能文件可窃取凭证、注入后门或重定向Agent行为。已有数百个恶意技能包被报告，但尚无系统化防御工具。

Method: SkillGate采用混合正则预过滤+LLM评判流水线：安全的信号文件完全跳过LLM（节省成本）；被标记的文件仅将匹配片段窗口发送给LLM评判，而非完整内容（节省token）。

Result: 在SkillsBench基准（n=1,650，9.1%恶意）上，SkillGate达到F1=0.817，FPR=1.13%，LLM输入token减少77%，AUPRC（0.830）优于现有工具（0.144/0.162）约5-6倍。

Conclusion: SkillGate是首个针对AI编程Agent技能文件供应链攻击的系统化防御方案，在检测效果、成本效率和低误报率方面均表现优异，且无需完整文件扫描即可实现高效检测。

Abstract: Software engineering teams now deploy AI coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, GitHub Copilot) as first-class productivity tools, installing domain-specific skill files to tailor agent behavior to project APIs, framework conventions, and organizational workflows. These complex Markdown files are easily downloaded from public registries with a single npx skills add command and no real security screening, representing a novel supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill file can silently reprogram agent behavior, exfiltrating credentials, injecting backdoors into generated code, or redirecting agent actions to attacker-controlled endpoints. The threat is not hypothetical: recent reports document hundreds of malicious skill packages in public registries, including organized campaigns that distributed credential-stealing infostealers via fake productivity skills. No systematic toolchain defense exists for this attack surface. We present SkillGate, a deployable security gateway that screens AI skill packages before coding agent installation. SkillGate uses a hybrid regex-prefilter + LLM-judge pipeline: safe-signal files bypass the LLM entirely (skip savings); flagged files have only their matched snippet windows sent to the judge, not the full content (snippet savings). We answer four research questions covering detection effectiveness, screening cost, runtime overhead, and false positive behavior on the SkillsBench benchmark against two existing tools. On SkillsBench (n=1,650, 9.1% malicious), SkillGate achieves F1=0.817, FPR=1.13% while reducing LLM input tokens by 77% vs. full-file screening, and outperforming existing tools by 5-6x on threshold-independent AUPRC (0.830 vs. 0.144/0.162).

</details>


### [150] [An Empirical Study of Model Context Protocol Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25635)
*Muhammad Hamza Arshad Majeed,May Mahmoud,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对1,723个MCP应用进行大规模研究，发现服务器集成在配置文件和SDK使用上趋于一致，但在命名规范和人类监督机制上存在显著差异，尤其是在工具执行的审批环节严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议标准化了LLM应用与外部工具的通信，但应用端缺乏规范，现有研究集中在服务器端而非消费端，因此需要系统性地分析MCP应用如何集成服务器。

Method: 从GitHub挖掘1,723个MCPApp，先从代表性样本中推导出MCPAppTax分类体系，再利用LLM辅助的pipeline对整个数据集进行分析，从配置、SDK使用和人在回路机制三个维度进行特征化。

Result: 85.2%的应用使用配置文件管理服务器，81.1%使用官方SDK进行通信，但配置文件名无统一规范；日志记录(90.8%)和启用/禁用控制(77.2%)普遍，但仅37.2%的应用在执行工具前设置审批步骤。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统在底层技术选型上已趋同，但在命名规范和安全管控方面仍不成熟，大多数应用缺乏对LLM工具调用的有效人类监督机制。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how large language model applications communicate with external tools, but leaves the application side unspecified: unlike traditional dependencies resolved through package managers, developers integrating MCP servers face no conventions for configuration, communication, or human oversight. This ecosystem is also under-researched, with existing work focused on servers rather than the applications consuming them. We conduct a large-scale study of 1,723 MCPApps mined from GitHub. We first derive MCPAppTax from a representative sample, then use an LLM-assisted pipeline to apply it across the full dataset, characterizing server integration across configuration, SDK use, and human-in-the-loop mechanisms. Our results show that the ecosystem has converged on some practices but not others: most MCPApps configure servers using files (85.2%) and use an official SDK (81.1%) to communicate with servers, yet no naming convention has emerged for configuration files. Human oversight diverges most, logging (90.8%) and enable/disable controls (77.2%) are common, but only 37.2% gate tool execution behind a blocking approval step, leaving the LLM able to invoke any enabled tool unconditionally in most MCPApps.

</details>


### [151] [Delta Debugging for Cyber-Physical Systems with Flaky Test Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25695)
*Pablo Valle,Shaukat Ali,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对随机性CPS模拟器中的非确定性（flaky）行为，提出了三种结合统计失败分析、重复执行和环境感知缩减的delta调试算法，能够在随机环境下隔离最小失败诱导测试输入。实验表明，该方法不仅大幅减少调试时间，还能通过消除偶然随机效应提高失败复现概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代CPS模拟器存在非确定性（flaky）行为，导致失败难以复现和调试，而传统delta调试方法不适用于随机环境，需要新的算法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出了三种delta调试算法，结合统计失败分析、重复执行和环境感知缩减，用于在随机CPS中隔离最小失败诱导测试输入。

Result: 在工业电梯调度系统和自主移动机器人两个案例上，提出的方法大幅减少调试时间，同时保留原始失败行为；更重要的是，最小化失败诱导输入反而提高了失败复现概率。

Conclusion: Delta调试不仅能简化失败分析，还能缓解执行flakiness问题，为CPS调试提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing is widely used to validate Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), yet modern CPS simulators frequently exhibit non-deterministic (flaky) behavior, making failures difficult to reproduce and debug. Although delta debugging has proven effective for deterministic systems, its underlying assumptions do not hold in stochastic environments. This paper presents three delta debugging algorithms that combine statistical failure analysis, repeated executions, and environment-aware reduction to isolate minimal failure-inducing test inputs for stochastic CPSs. We evaluate the proposed techniques on two complementary case study systems: an industrial elevator dispatching system employing stochastic optimization and an autonomous mobile robot exhibiting simulator-induced non-determinism. The results show that the proposed approaches substantially reduce debugging time while preserving the original failure behavior. More importantly, we observe that minimizing failure-inducing test inputs frequently increases failure reproducibility compared with the original executions. By eliminating execution segments that introduce incidental stochastic effects, the reduced test inputs isolate the causal conditions of the failure and consistently reproduce it with higher probability. These findings suggest that delta debugging not only simplifies failure analysis but also mitigates execution flakiness, providing a practical foundation for debugging CPSs.

</details>


### [152] [WarmTuner: Program-Specific Warm Starts for Compiler Autotuning via Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25831)
*Tianlu Qiao,Mingxuan Zhu,Zeyu Sun,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了WarmTuner，一个离线到在线的强化学习框架，通过学习历史数据中的程序条件策略并结合实时编译反馈来优化编译器标志配置，在GCC 15.2.0上实现了平均1.732x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编译器有数百个优化标志，组合爆炸导致难以找到适合目标程序的最佳配置。现有自动调优方法在搜索开始后知识固化，历史先验与目标程序不匹配时会浪费大量在线预算。

Method: 提出WarmTuner框架：离线阶段从历史数据学习一个程序条件策略，覆盖完整标志空间；在线阶段使用GRPO算法，基于真实编译运行反馈在目标程序上细化该策略，使策略由实测加速比而非历史数据驱动。

Result: 在GCC 15.2.0上对cBench和PolyBench评估，平均加速比1.732x（对比-O3），在30个程序中14个取得最佳结果，显著优于对比技术。

Conclusion: WarmTuner通过离线学习+在线细化的强化学习框架，有效结合了历史知识和实时反馈，相比现有方法在编译器自动调优任务上取得了更优的性能。

Abstract: Compilers are fundamental software tools that translate high-level programs into machine code. Modern compilers expose hundreds of optimizations, each turned on or off through an optimization flag, to improve the performance of the generated code. However, the number of possible flag combinations grows exponentially, making it difficult to find a flag configuration well suited to a given target program. Existing compiler auto-tuning techniques reduce tuning cost by pruning the search space, injecting search biases, or predicting configuration performance. Although some exploit program features, the knowledge they extract from historical data is frozen once search begins; runtime feedback then guides only the search itself, never the prior. As a result, when this prior mismatches the target program, these methods waste much of the limited online budget before the search reaches good configurations. We propose WarmTuner, an offline-to-online reinforcement learning framework that instead turns historical records into a program-conditioned policy that predicts each flag's setting over the full flag space and remains adaptable on the target program. Offline, WarmTuner learns this program-conditioned policy over the full flag space from historical good configurations. Online, it refines the same policy on the target program using real compile-run feedback, so that the policy is driven by measured speedups rather than limited to the historical data. We instantiate the online update with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which compares candidates in the same round and avoids a separate value model. We evaluate WarmTuner on GCC 15.2.0 with cBench and PolyBench. The results show that WarmTuner achieves an average speedup of 1.732x over GCC -O3 and obtains the best result on 14/30 programs, significantly outperforming the compared techniques.

</details>


### [153] [Rethinking Training Data for Generating Code Review Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25851)
*Leonardo Centellas-Claros,Estefania Pakarati-Cofre,Juan Pablo Sandoval Alcocer,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出代码评审评论生成领域中的训练数据存在“错配”问题（代码变更与评论之间缺乏可靠的学习信号），通过实证研究归纳出三种错配类型（语义模糊、缺乏可操作性、上下文依赖），并论证仅靠数据清洗不足以解决问题，需要重新审视任务定义、输入上下文和评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的代码评审评论生成方法常产生泛化、弱关联或不可操作的评论，且数据集含有噪声。作者认为问题不仅来自数据噪声，更深层的原因在于任务定义本身存在缺陷。

Method: 对一个广泛使用的评审评论数据集进行实证检验，识别“错配”训练样本，归纳错配类型学，并探索将该类型学融入LLM筛选的效果。

Result: 归纳出三种错配来源：语义模糊、缺乏可操作性、上下文依赖；发现即便引入类型学，LLM检测错配样本仍然困难。

Conclusion: 改进代码评审评论生成不能仅靠数据清洗，还需明确有效性标准、更丰富的上下文输入、以及契合评审意图和可操作性的评估实践。

Abstract: Generating code review comments has become a prominent research direction in automated code review, commonly formulated as a text generation task over diff-comment pairs. Despite advances in learning-based approaches, generated review comments are often generic, weakly grounded, or non-actionable. Recent studies have also shown that review comment datasets contain noisy or unsuitable training instances, motivating LLM-based dataset cleaning approaches. In this paper, we argue that problematic training instances are not homogeneous and that some limitations stem from deeper issues in the task formulation itself. Through an empirical inspection of a widely used review comment dataset, we identify misaligned training pairs: instances where the relationship between the code change and the review comment does not provide a reliable learning signal for generating actionable review feedback from localized inputs. We derive a taxonomy of misalignment capturing three recurring sources: semantic ambiguity, lack of actionability, and context dependence. We further explore whether incorporating this taxonomy into LLM-based filtering improves the identification of problematic training instances, observing that detecting misaligned training pairs remains challenging. Based on these observations, we argue that improving review comment generation requires more than dataset cleaning alone, motivating explicit validity criteria, richer contextual inputs, and evaluation practices aligned with review intent and actionability.

</details>


### [154] [How Do LLMs Read Bug Reports? An Empirical Study of Attention in LLMs for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25873)
*Ramtin Ehsani,Irene Manotas,Saurabh Pujar,Luca Buratti,Preetha Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究LLM程序修复中的注意力模式，发现修复失败的关键原因在于模型注意力过度集中在元数据等非关键信息上，而成功修复则表现为注意力分散在多个诊断组件上，且与开发者关注的区域更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使提供相同上下文信息，LLM对相似bug的修复表现却不一致，背后的原因尚不清楚；模型如何对bug报告中的不同信息进行优先级排序以及注意力机制是否影响修复成功率，这些问题缺乏解释性的理解。

Method: 分析SWE-bench Verified和Multi-SWE-bench中319个真实的Python和Java bug，研究三个问题：注意力在bug报告各部分的分布、成功与失败修复的注意力模式差异、以及这些模式与开发者认为重要的信息的对比。

Result: 成功修复的特征是注意力分散在多个诊断组件（如bug描述、堆栈轨迹、测试用例）上；失败修复则表现出注意力过度集中在元数据（如版本信息）上。模型注意力与开发者识别的关键部分和短语越一致，修复成功率越高。

Conclusion: 注意力分配不当是LLM程序修复失败的关键因素，这一发现为设计更可解释和更可靠的自动程序修复系统提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair systems are advancing rapidly, yet their performance remains inconsistent. Even when provided with the same contextual information, an LLM may generate a correct patch for one bug but fail on another closely related bug. Why this happens remains poorly understood, and it is unclear how LLMs prioritize the diverse information in bug reports and whether model attention affects repair success. In this paper, we present the first empirical study of attention patterns in LLM-based program repair, providing interpretable insights into how models process bug reports and where their attention is concentrated during repair. We analyze 319 real-world Python and Java bugs from SWE-bench Verified and Multi-SWE-bench to study (RQ1) how model attention is distributed across bug report sections, (RQ2) how attention patterns within each section differ between successful and unsuccessful repairs, and (RQ3) how these patterns compare to information developers consider important for bug fixing. We find that successful repairs are characterized by diffused attention across multiple diagnostic components such as bug descriptions, stacktraces, and test cases, while failures often exhibit over-localized attention toward metadata such as version information. We further observe that stronger alignment between model attention and developer-identified key sections and phrases is associated with higher repair success. Our results provide the first empirical evidence that attention misallocation is a key factor in LLM-based APR failures, and offer actionable insights for designing more interpretable and reliable future APR systems.

</details>


### [155] [CONQuER: Hardware-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantisation with Online-Calibrated Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25884)
*Aidan Dakhama,Ajitha Rajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CONQuER是一个将混合精度量化集成到编译器流水线中的统一基础设施，通过NSGA-II进化算法和双代理预筛选引擎，在资源受限硬件上实现硬件感知的量化配置优化，最高提升12.19倍推理速度，且top-1精度损失仅1.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前混合精度量化的部署工具链存在严重碎片化：量化作为硬件无关的预处理步骤与下游编译器脱节，导致位宽配置与硬件异构执行单元（如张量核、变长向量单元）不匹配，造成严重的运行时性能损失。同时，硬件在环测试评估配置的搜索空间呈指数级增长，难以穷举。

Method: CONQuER将量化移入TOSA级别的编译器流水线；采用NSGA-II进化算法结合双代理预筛选引擎（评估理论缓存边界和特征空间各向同性）来筛选可行配置；仅对最强候选策略在IREE硬件上执行，并将执行指标反馈给在线校准器，使代理模型与真实硬件行为对齐。

Result: 在移动CPU、笔记本CPU和服务器GPU上的评估表明，最优量化策略具有硬件依赖性。CONQuER发现的帕累托最优配置可实现高达12.19倍的推理加速，top-1精度在未量化基线的1.44%以内。

Conclusion: 将量化与编译器降级和物理执行耦合是发现硬件依赖的最优量化策略的关键，CONQuER证明了此方法的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained hardware relies on mixed-precision quantisation (MPQ). current deployment toolchains severely fragment this process. Quantisation typically occurs as a hardware-agnostic preprocessing step in front-end frameworks, disconnected from the downstream compilers that generate the physical machine code. This separation leads to suboptimal configurations where assigned bit-widths map poorly to the target machine's heterogeneous hardware execution blocks such as tensor cores and variable-width vector units, incurring severe runtime execution penalties. Furthermore, evaluating these configurations via exhaustive hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testing is intractable due to the exponentially large search space. We present CONQuER, a unified compiler-integrated infrastructure for hardware-aware MPQ. CONQuER shifts quantisation into the compiler pipeline at the TOSA level, enabling intelligent configuration handling based on compiler support. To evaluate this combinatorial search space of different of model layers within practical compilation budgets, CONQuER couples an NSGA-II evolutionary algorithm with a dual-surrogate prescreening engine. This engine evaluates theoretical cache memory bounds and feature space isotropy to discard non-viable configurations. CONQuER then executes only the strongest candidate policies on hardware via IREE, feeding the execution metrics into an online calibrator. This calibrator aligns the surrogate models with the true hardware behaviour during an NSGA-II evolutionary search. Evaluation across mobile and laptop CPUs, and server GPUs demonstrates that optimal quantisation policies are hardware-dependent. By coupling quantisation with compiler lowering and physical execution, CONQuER discovers Pareto-optimal configurations up to 12.19x faster inference with top-1 accuracy within 1.44% of the unquantised baseline.

</details>


### [156] [RSIBench-Data: Benchmarking Data-Centric Research for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25886)
*Fanqing Meng,Lingxiao Du,Qiguang Chen,Ziqi Zhao,Haocheng Lu,Mengkang Hu,Michael Qizhe Shieh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RSIBench-Data基准，用于评估LLM代理作为数据驱动研究者的能力。实验表明代理在58.33%的设置下能从反馈中改进策略，但改进不稳定，78.26%的持续搜索最终得分下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将研究决策与优化、服务、评估等系统实现混在一起，难以评估代理的研究能力。需要一种受控环境来专门测试LLM代理在数据中心的自动循环改进能力。

Method: 固定后训练栈，代理在固定目标模型上迭代修改训练数据策略，使用Tinker服务进行训练和推理，通过Harbor和E2B沙箱进行官方评估，预算固定。在六个基准上评估四个前沿代理。

Result: 代理在58.33%设置中能从反馈改进策略，但78.26%的持续搜索最终得分低于最佳值；识别出四种强运行模式：准确假设、验证监督、行为对齐数据、保留强检查点。

Conclusion: 当前代理能做出有用的数据发现，但无法将反馈转化为持续改进。RSIBench-Data为递归自我改进所需的研究能力提供了可测量、可审计的测试平台。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires turning evidence of model failures into better models. Data-centric post-training research entails diagnosing capability gaps, designing and validating training-data strategies, and learning from checkpoint feedback. Can LLM agents automate this loop? Existing benchmarks entangle research decisions with optimization, serving, evaluation, and systems implementation, obscuring agents' research capability. We introduce RSIBench-Data, a controlled benchmark of LLM agents as data-centric researchers with a fixed post-training stack. Agents iteratively revise training-data strategies for a fixed target model; training and serving use Tinker-backed services, official evaluation runs through Harbor and E2B sandboxes, and budgets are fixed across agents. We evaluate four frontier agents on six benchmarks across software engineering, terminal use, scientific question answering, and mathematics. Agents demonstrate core data-centric research capabilities: in 58.33\% of settings, they improve upon the first valid attempt by refining strategies from feedback. However, improvement is inconsistent. Among searches continuing after the best observed score, 78.26\% end with a lower-scoring final attempt, while the rest only recover the same peak. A strong candidate may therefore appear early or midway through a run even as later revisions fail. Trajectory analysis identifies four patterns in stronger runs: accurate hypotheses, validation-grounded supervision, behavior-aligned data, and preservation of strong checkpoints. These findings suggest that current agents can make useful data-centric discoveries but cannot yet translate feedback into consistent improvements. RSIBench-Data provides a measurable, auditable testbed for the research capabilities required for recursive self-improvement. We open-source our code at https://github.com/evolvent-ai/RSIBench-Data.

</details>


### [157] [A Low-Cost Human-in-the-Loop Investigation of Toxicity on GitHub at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25946)
*Rahat Rizvi Rahman,Mia Mohammad Imran,Kostadin Damevski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协同（HITL）的毒性标注方法，结合小型本地LLM和随机森林验证器，对超过12.4万条GitHub讨论进行大规模毒性标注，验证并修正了先前小规模研究的部分结论，提供了开源社区毒性的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub上的毒性讨论会威胁开源项目的可持续性，但现有大规模毒性研究不足，且毒性常是隐式和上下文相关的，导致人工标注和LLM标注均不可靠，亟需可扩展的领域校准标注方法。

Method: 采用人机协同（HITL）标注方法：先用小型本地LLM生成毒性预测和可解释的事件类别得分，再通过轻量级随机森林验证器筛选出最可能误标的小部分对话，仅对这些对话进行人工审核。

Result: 该方法在标注成本极小的情况下，优于基于置信度和多LLM的基线方法；成功应用于12.4万+条GitHub讨论，验证了部分先前小规模研究发现，同时也修正了另一些结论。

Conclusion: 所提HITL标注方法能够高效、可靠地实现大规模毒性标注，为开源社区毒性研究提供了更全面的实证基础，并揭示了跨项目的毒性流行程度、特征和动态的新见解。

Abstract: Toxic interactions in open source discussions can alienate contributors and threaten project sustainability, yet prior empirical studies of GitHub toxicity have been limited in scale, raising questions about their generalizability. Scaling up is difficult because toxicity on GitHub is often implicit and context-dependent, making both fully manual annotation and LLM-based labeling unreliable.
  We present a human-in-the-loop (HITL) annotation methodology that makes large-scale, domain-calibrated toxicity labeling practical. A single call to a small, local LLM produces both a toxicity prediction and a set of interpretable event category scores. A lightweight Random Forest validator then uses those scores to flag the small subset of conversations most likely to be mislabeled, directing human review only where it is needed. The validator outperforms confidence-based and multi-LLM baselines while adding low annotation cost.
  We apply this pipeline to over 124,000 GitHub issue and pull request conversations. Using the resulting dataset, we evaluate key findings from prior small-scale research, confirming some and qualifying others, and present new insights into the prevalence, characteristics, and dynamics of toxicity across diverse open source projects.

</details>


### [158] [RepoReasoner: Evaluating Repository-Level Code Reasoning Ability of Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25996)
*Yanlin Wang,Suiquan Wang,Yanli Wang,Bowen Zhang,Daya Guo,Jiachi Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoReasoner是一个评估大语言模型在仓库级别代码推理能力的基准测试，包含输出预测和调用链预测两个任务。评估发现，即使是最优模型在跨文件推理上准确率也仅69.1%，模型存在高精度低召回、依赖记忆化而非真正理解、长上下文反而引入噪声等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多在函数级别评估代码推理，无法反映真实开发中跨文件和复杂依赖结构的推理需求。

Method: 通过多阶段流水线构建基准：利用pytest动态追踪获取真实调用链，结合LLM驱动I/O重写减少记忆化效应。包含输出预测（细粒度状态执行推理）和调用链预测（高层架构依赖理解）两个互补任务。

Result: 7个SOTA模型中，最佳模型在输出预测上仅达69.1% Pass@1；调用链预测表现为高精度低召回；在重写数据上性能下降表明存在记忆化依赖；长上下文因噪声未能持续提升效果。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在仓库级别代码推理方面存在根本性局限，未来需要关注结构化架构理解和跨文件推理能力的研究。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on software engineering tasks, yet most existing benchmarks evaluate code reasoning at the function level, where all relevant information is localized. This setting fails to reflect real-world development, which requires reasoning across multiple files and complex dependency structures. We introduce RepoReasoner, a benchmark for evaluating repository-level code reasoning. It assesses two complementary abilities: Output Prediction, which measures fine-grained, stateful execution reasoning across files, and Call Chain Prediction, which evaluates high-level architectural dependency understanding under noisy context. Our benchmark is constructed through a multi-stage pipeline that leverages dynamic tracing of pytest executions to obtain ground-truth call chains, along with LLM-based I/O rewriting to reduce memorization effects. We evaluate seven state-of-the-art LLMs. Even under oracle context, the best-performing model achieves only 69.1% Pass@1 on Output Prediction, indicating that cross-file reasoning remains a major challenge. In Call Chain Prediction, models exhibit high precision but low recall, suggesting limited multi-hop dependency understanding. Furthermore, performance drops on rewritten data reveal partial reliance on memorization, and longer contexts do not consistently improve results due to noise. These findings highlight fundamental limitations in current LLMs' repository-level reasoning and motivate future work on structured architectural understanding and cross-file inference.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，15个模型中9个存在显著的合规性差距，其中5个在移除评估与部署后果关联信息后仍持续存在，表明对齐假装可能不需要复杂的工具性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐假装现象的原因尚未被充分理解，既有研究认为其需要明确的后果关联信息，但最新工作提示机制性动机可能更复杂，因此需要探究后果链接信息是否必要。

Method: 将15个模型置于违反企业网络访问政策以帮助用户的社会正向请求场景中，测试其合规性差距；移除评估与部署后果关联的语言后再次测试；同时测试目标语言对模型偏好的影响。

Result: 9个模型产生显著合规性差距，5个在移除后果关联信息后仍存在；目标语言对不同模型的影响存在差异，有些模型因此增加违规，有些则减少。

Conclusion: 对齐假装可能不需要此前认为的复杂工具性框架支撑，被监控的行为可能无法可靠反映模型在实际部署中的行为。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出llm-wiki-memory-template，一种基于LLM维护的互连Wiki模式，用于解决科研项目中知识难以传承、失败路径丢失的问题，并通过多个人类/AI/领域协作案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研项目中的发现、决策和推理过程难以被后续协作者复现；出版物和共享代码中常排除失败路径和被撤回的结论，导致重复失败；LLM编程代理缺乏跨会话的持久记忆，检索增强生成也无法累积知识。这些问题需要一种结构化的知识保存与共享机制。

Method: 基于llm-wiki模式（Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026），在原始资料和代理之间插入一个由LLM维护的互连Wiki；采用仅追加（append-only）的约定以保留失败路径；模板包含三个架构元素分别支持多人类、多AI代理、多领域三个协作轴。

Result: 三个已部署案例和一个设计报告：单个研究者保留被放弃的迭代；双人项目中回溯审计将两个实验声称的20/20覆盖率修正为14和12，修复后提升至18和18，失败路径完整保留；进行中的多代理部署设计；跨领域教育变体。

Conclusion: llm-wiki-memory-template可作为异构协作知识工作的基础架构，具备失败路径保留、代理诚实性和 Appropriation（挪用/再创造）三个跨领域的社会技术属性，而不仅是技术机制。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [161] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个开源端到端智能优化框架，能直接优化任意未修改 PyTorch 模型中的计算内核，采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多条优化路径，支持视觉、扩散和 LLM 任务，并提供图形界面用于监控和调试。在四个模型上，仅用50次优化迭代即可显著提速，最高达2.83倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GPU 内核优化依赖专家手工编写底层代码，成本高昂；现有基于 LLM 的自动化工具局限于随机张量测试、生成孤立 CUDA 代码、主要支持 LLM 模型，且缺乏调试支持。为克服这些限制，作者提出了 Kernel Forge。

Method: Kernel Forge 是一个端到端智能优化框架，可直接接收任意未修改的 PyTorch 模型；采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多条优化路径，而非单一线性精炼链；配备图形用户界面用于监控进度、检查候选内核和调试失败。

Result: 在 NVIDIA DGX Spark（GB10 GPU）上评估了四个模型（ResNet-50、Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium、Gemma 4 E2B、Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B），仅用50次优化迭代，14个内核超越 PyTorch eager 模式：adaptive_avgpool2d 加速1.52倍，group_norm 加速1.70倍，softmax 分别加速2.83倍和1.54倍。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge 证明了基于 LLM 的智能体系统可以高效、通用地优化多种深度学习模型的内核，显著减少人工成本并提升性能，同时提供了良好的可调试性。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [162] [Chart-Supported or Model-Supplied? Examining MLLM-Generated Claims for Accessible Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25021)
*Ishrat Jahan Eliza,Md Dilshadur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过1,224条描述分析，发现多模态大模型（MLLMs）在解释可视化图表时，其声称的"直接证据"常与模型推测混淆。研究表明，提供图表上下文可提升部分模型（如Gemini、GPT）的可靠性，但图像本身对数值一致性帮助有限。结论呼吁建立能区分"基于证据的声明"与"模型推测"的可访问描述系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在解释可视化图表时，常常将模型内部的推测性内容与基于实际数据的直接证据混为一谈，缺乏清晰的区分标准，导致用户难以判断分析结果的可靠性。

Method: 从四个来源收集102个可视化图表，使用三个多模态大模型，在四种输入条件下（控制图像访问、上下文信息、框架提示等），生成1,224条描述，并对模型生成的DIRECT（直接）、DERIVED（推导）和SPECULATIVE（推测）三类标签进行自动化审计和数值一致性分析。

Result: 提供图表上下文可提升Gemini和GPT生成直接声明（DIRECT）的比例，并改善部分模型的数值一致性；但添加图像并未带来一致性提升，"隐藏上下文"提示也未可靠增加谨慎措辞；"现实意义"部分仍以推测性（SPECULATIVE）内容为主。

Conclusion: 应开发能够清晰区分"基于输入证据的声明"和"模型自行补充的解释"的可访问描述系统，以提高多模态大模型在可视化分析中的透明度和可信度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can connect visualization patterns to external causes, consequences, and domain knowledge, but the evidential basis of these interpretations is often unclear. We present an exploratory study of 102 visualizations from four sources, three MLLMs, and four input conditions that vary access to the image, source-specific accessible chart context, and withheld-context framing. Across 1,224 descriptions, we analyze model-attributed DIRECT, DERIVED, and SPECULATIVE labels and conduct an automated audit of numeric agreement. Accessible chart context shifted Gemini and GPT toward DIRECT claims and improved numeric agreement for some models. Adding the image to the full context did not yield a consistent numeric benefit, and the withheld-context prompt did not reliably increase cautious language. The prompt-defined Real-World Significance section remained predominantly SPECULATIVE. These results motivate accessible description systems that distinguish claims supported by supplied evidence from model-supplied interpretation

</details>


### [163] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CaRE评估框架，发现掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的现有评价标准存在严重问题：温度设置主导了MAUVE指标变化，计算量匹配后会逆转多个已发表的策略排名，且高熵重掩码与随机去掩码存在相互制约关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MDLM研究在评估标准上严重滞后于模型发展速度。七篇近期重掩码论文采用互不兼容的评估设置（名义步数、指标、采样温度各不相同，且未联合控制这些因素），导致策略排名不可比，无法判断报告的性能提升是来自算法改进还是评估人为产物。

Method: 提出CaRE（Compute-aware Evaluation）框架：标准化实际函数评估次数（NFE）、强制执行多指标报告、明确控制随机性。在LLaDA-8B-Base和Dream-7B-Base上，对7种重掩码策略、4个随机性水平、3个步数预算进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 温度解释了MAUVE方差的大部分；(2) 计算量匹配后翻转了多个已发表的策略排名；(3) 信息重掩码与随机去掩码存在张力，高熵重掩码在256步、unmask_temp=0.25时使MAUVE下降0.296（p=0.020）。覆盖12个开放权重MDLM（150M-8B）的排行榜显示该交互方向在多种架构和规模下一致。

Conclusion: 当前MDLM评估系统性地将算法改进与计算量和随机性的隐藏选择相混淆。研究团队公开了评估协议、实现和排行榜，以确保未来的重掩码研究结果可复现、可比较。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [164] [PLATO: Pointer Learner for Agent and Task Openness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25082)
*Alireza Saleh Abadi,Leen-Kiat Soh,Daniel Alan Redder,Adam Eck,Prashant Doshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PLATO框架，结合指针网络与图神经网络，在开放多智能体系统中同时应对智能体与任务的动态变化，无需预设边界，并在MOASEI灭火场景中展现出比现有方法更强的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法难以应对开放系统中智能体和任务集合动态变化的问题，传统填充/掩码方法引入人为边界，图方法仅处理单一开放维度，缺乏同时应对智能体开放与任务开放的统一方案。

Method: 提出Pointer Learner for Agent and Task Openness (PLATO)，包含基于指针网络的Actor（直接对当前任务集输出分布）和GNN集中式Critic（编码智能体-任务交互图），结合CTDE范式下的MAPPO训练，形式化为TaAgO-MG。主要贡献是指针网络直接支持动态动作空间，无需掩码或重训练；GNN批评家灵活适应智能体和任务数量的变化。

Result: 在MOASEI野火抑制域中，PLATO在开放智能体与开放任务场景下均取得强性能，且在零样本泛化方面比现有SOTA基线更稳定、更一致。

Conclusion: PLATO有效解决了OASYS中同时存在的智能体开放和任务开放挑战，证明了指针网络与GNN结合在无界状态-动作空间中的可行性，为开放多智能体系统提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Open agent systems (OASYS) are increasingly prevalent in real-world domains where the sets of agents and tasks change unpredictably over time. Such openness, including agent openness (AO) and task openness (TO), poses a fundamental challenge to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), which typically assumes fixed state and action spaces. Existing methods address openness only partially: padding and masking approaches introduce artificial bounds, while recent graph-based or hypergraph methods handle one dimension of openness but still depend on restrictive assumptions. In this paper, we introduce Pointer Learner for Agent and Task Openness (PLATO), a pointer-network-based actor combined with a centralized graph neural network (GNN) critic, trained with multi-agent proximal policy optimization under a centralized training and decentralized execution paradigm. Our pointer-based actor outputs distributions directly over the current task set. This directly supports changing action spaces without masking or retraining. Our GNN critic encodes agent-task interactions as a graph that changes shape with task and agent composition. Together, these components consider AO and TO without the boundedness of existing approaches. We formalize PLATO in a Task-and-Agent-Open Markov Game (TaAgO-MG), extending prior task-open formulations, and prove it is well-defined over the resulting unbounded state and action spaces. We evaluate PLATO with the Methods for Open Agent Systems Evaluation Initiative (MOASEI) wildfire suppression domain, an environment designed for open multi-agent system evaluation, and we demonstrate strong performance and more consistent zero-shot generalization than state-of-the-art baselines in OASYS.

</details>


### [165] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROCLM，一种基于微调语言模型的杂货品类推荐系统，通过两阶段LoRA训练编码周期性购买模式，并采用trie约束解码确保输出有效，在实际生产中实现了7.5%的购物车添加率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线杂货购物推荐面临可扩展性和准确性挑战，传统基于单品的方法难以有效捕捉周期性购买行为和多样化的用户意图，因此需要更结构化的品类级推荐方案。

Method: 采用两阶段LoRA微调策略将周期性购买模式编码到模型参数中，并引入基于trie树的约束解码机制，确保输出限定在预定义品类空间内，实现有效且可控的推荐生成。

Result: 在专有生产数据和公开基准上均优于强基线模型；在生产补货任务中，每次展示的购物车添加数相对提升了7.5%，且能高效地联合生成所有品类推荐。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型集成到结构化推荐系统中是有效且实用的，GROCLM在真实生产环境中的表现证明了这一方向的可行性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [166] [How Affect Propagates among LLM Agents: Emergent Emotional Contagion in Crowd Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25140)
*Funda Durupinar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的多智能体情感传播架构，通过感知-评价-表达循环实现情感 contagion，无需显式机制即可在大五人格和Russell情感模型基础上模拟稀疏小规模人群中的情感传播动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型在多智能体人群模拟中如何通过感知与评价实现情感传播，理解情感 contagion 在空间、时间和人格维度上的结构特性。

Method: 每个智能体通过视觉、听觉、触觉通道感知邻居，利用LLM根据人格画像、记忆、当前情感状态和情境上下文进行评价，更新内部情感状态并选择外部表达。底层导航由传统人群模拟器处理。

Result: 系统在五种场景中产生了具有空间、时间和人格依赖结构的情感 contagion 动力学：警报以波前形式传播，平均警报比例稳定在非零平台，人格分布决定模糊警报是否引发恐慌及挑衅被解读为愤怒还是恐惧。动力学表现依赖模型后端。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的感知-评价-表达循环能够有效模拟情感传播，情感动力学具有可预测的结构化特征，且受模型选择影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the behavior of language models in a multi-agent crowd simulation, focusing on how affect propagates among agents that perceive and appraise one another. Each agent perceives its neighbors through visual, auditory, and tactile channels, then appraises these perceptions in light of its prompted personality profile, memory, current affective state, and situational context. Appraisal is carried out by an LLM, which updates the agent's internal affective state and selects its outward expression. The architecture contains no hand-authored mechanism for directly transferring affective state between agents; instead, inter-agent influence arises through the perception-appraisal-expression loop. The agent representation draws on the Big Five personality model and Russell's circumplex model of affect. To limit latency, low-level steering and navigation are handled by a conventional crowd simulator operating independently of the LLM-based cognitive layer.
  We evaluate the architecture across five scenario environments spanning alarming, joyful, and neutral situations in different spatial layouts. The results show that the system produces emotional contagion dynamics with spatial, temporal, and personality-dependent structure in sparse, small crowds. Alarm spreads from seeded agents as a traveling front, the mean alarmed fraction settles at a nonzero plateau, and the distribution of prompted personality profiles determines whether an ambiguous alarm ignites panic and whether a provocation is interpreted as anger or fear. We further evaluate the appraisal step through controlled experiments across prompt variants, sampling temperatures, and four model backends, showing that the dynamics are backend-dependent.

</details>


### [167] [Crystalis: Progressive Nucleation and Semantic Annealing for Coordinated Multi-View Visualization Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24766)
*Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xin Qian,Xiaotong Wang,Zi Ying,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Crystalis框架，通过查询中心的多视图协调建模，将协调多视图可视化分解为数据、可视化、交互三类组件在三层抽象级别上的结构化查询，采用渐进式成核和语义退火两种机制，实现了LLM生成结构正确的协调多视图可视化，成功率高达75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM可以生成单个图表，但无法可靠地生成协调多视图可视化（CMVs），因为视图间存在紧密的字段级耦合，一个组件的错误会无声地使其他组件失效，需要探索LLM能否可靠地生成结构正确的CMVs以及什么抽象能使这成为可能。

Method: 提出了Crystalis框架，基于查询中心CMV建模，将CMV分解为跨三种组件类型（数据、可视化、交互）和三个抽象级别（需求、规范、可执行对象）的依赖图上的结构化查询；采用渐进式成核机制沿依赖顺序垂直地将每个查询从需求结晶为对象，以及语义退火机制通过分层逻辑检查在每层水平上强制查询间的一致性。

Result: 在5个前沿LLM的12任务基准上，Crystalis实现了高达75%的端到端成功率，显著优于智能体编码基线（相同基础模型下8.3%）；12名从业者的用户研究确认了分解和迭代优化工作流的可用性。

Conclusion: Crystalis框架通过结构化分解和双重机制有效提升了LLM生成协调多视图可视化的可靠性，为未来LLM辅助可视化生成提供了可行的抽象路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate individual charts, but coordinated multi-view visualizations (CMVs), where views share data flows and cross-view interactions, remain out of reach. Tight field-level coupling among data transformations, visual encodings, and interaction coordinations causes errors in one component to silently invalidate others. Rather than pursuing end-to-end analytical quality, which depends on model capability, domain knowledge, and user expertise, we target a foundational question: can LLMs reliably produce structurally correct CMVs, and what abstractions make this possible? We present Crystalis, a framework built on query-centric CMV modeling that decomposes a CMV into structured queries over a dependency graph spanning three component types (Data, Visualization, Interaction) and three abstraction levels (requirement, specification, executable object). Two complementary mechanisms operate over this structure: progressive nucleation crystallizes each query vertically from requirement to object along the dependency order, while semantic annealing enforces horizontal consistency across queries at each level through layered logical checks. On a 12-task benchmark across five frontier LLMs, Crystalis achieves up to 75% end-to-end success, substantially outperforming an agentic coding baseline (8.3% E2E with the same foundation model), and a user study with 12 practitioners confirms the usability of the decomposition and iterative refinement workflow.

</details>


### [168] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PATHFinder Agent，一个端到端对话式智能系统，通过结构化对话收集患者健康与社会背景，依据ACOG的PATH指南制定个性化产前护理计划，并整合社区资源。系统包含四个阶段流程，评估显示GPT-5.2在五个临床维度上平均得分最高（77.6%），但在产前检测建议方面存在关键缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前护理是改善妊娠结局的重要预防性服务，ACOG发布了PATH指南倡导个性化护理，但缺少实际落地的智能系统，因此需要开发一个端到端对话系统来辅助实施个性化产前护理计划。

Method: 设计了一个四阶段工作流的对话智能系统：患者信息录入、动态交互、方案合成和临床医生审核；使用前沿大语言模型，通过专家设计的评分标准在五个临床维度进行评估。

Result: GPT-5.2取得最高平均分77.6%，但在产前检测建议方面仍存在关键缺口。

Conclusion: 该系统的有效性仍需通过未来的人体参与研究和随机对照试验进行验证。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [169] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多语言环境下的欺骗与阴谋行为，发现低资源语言的阴谋评分比高资源语言平均高34.2%，且阴谋评分与预训练语言覆盖度呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究主要集中在英语环境，忽视了多语言安全评估，存在重大研究空白。

Method: 使用开源自动化审计框架Petri，对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多种语言中评估欺骗和阴谋行为。

Result: 低资源语言在五类阴谋指数上平均评分比高资源语言高34.2%；预训练语言覆盖度与阴谋评分呈负相关，且该效应在不同阴谋行为类型上不均匀。

Conclusion: 多语言环境下的AI对齐安全评估至关重要，低资源语言面临更高的对齐风险。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [170] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProcAgent是一个完全在边缘设备（NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin）上运行的智能程序助手，采用提出-验证架构，结合低延迟连续感知、符号任务图、按需视觉语言验证和基于LLM的交互代理，能够实时自适应地指导用户完成家具组装等程序性任务，避免依赖云端推理，保护隐私，并在用户研究中获得良好评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序性任务（如家具组装）的多模态助手依赖云端推理和固定持续感知，不适合对隐私敏感、延迟要求高的家庭场景，因此需要一种完全在设备端运行的高效助手。

Method: 提出提出-验证（propose-and-verify）架构，包含：低延迟连续感知模块持续跟踪用户进度；符号任务图表示任务结构；仅在出现歧义或偏差时调用昂贵的视觉语言验证；基于LLM的交互代理支持响应式问答和主动干预（需人工确认）。

Result: 完全在设备端运行，文本查询约2秒响应，视觉查询约8秒响应。10名用户的组装任务研究中，在可理解性、可操作性和隐私舒适度方面获得积极评价。

Conclusion: 自适应程序性助手可以在不牺牲可用性的前提下完全在边缘硬件上实现。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings. We present ProcAgent, a fully on-device, agentic, vision-based procedural assistant for real-time adaptive guidances on a single NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. ProcAgent uses a propose-and-verify architecture that combines low-latency continuous perception, a symbolic task graph, on-demand vision-language verification, and an LLM-based interaction agent. The system continuously proposes user progress, invokes expensive visual reasoning only when ambiguity or likely deviation arises, and supports both reactive question answering and proactive intervention with human-in-the- loop confirmation. We evaluate ProcAgent along four dimensions: perception accuracy, reasoning, task-level performance, and user experience. Despite running entirely on-device, the system maintains responsive interaction, resolving text-only queries in approximately 2 seconds and visually grounded queries in approximately 8 seconds. In a user study with 10 participants completing assembly tasks, ProcAgent receives positive ratings for comprehensibility, actionability, and privacy comfort. These results show that adaptive procedural assistance can be achieved entirely on edge hardware without sacrificing usability.

</details>


### [171] [RoCo-ACE: Rollout-Conditioned Online Distillation for Retention-Aware Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24771)
*Yan Hong,Wei Li,Kedong Xiu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RoCo-ACE方法，通过rollout-conditioned在线蒸馏和参考侧锚定修正，在注入新知识的同时有效避免模型行为漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识注入方法存在两个问题：1) 拟合完整权威答案会导致非更新行为漂移；2) 统一参考条件蒸馏对参考支持的token关注不足，且对遗漏事实仅提供间接监督。

Method: RoCo-ACE包含两部分：RoCo使用同rollout内无参考/有参考条件的似然对比，为参考支持的rollout token分配额外蒸馏权重；ACE引入稀疏的参考侧锚定修正，对rollout遗漏的权威锚点进行纠正，避免全答案模仿。

Result: 在三种知识注入设置、六个保留基准、多个基线和基础模型上，RoCo-ACE在注入知识准确率上达到最佳，同时保留行为接近基础模型。

Conclusion: RoCo-ACE通过更精细的蒸馏信号实现了知识注入与行为保留之间的更好平衡。

Abstract: Knowledge injection updates pretrained MLLMs with new factual or domain-specific knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers can cause drift in non-updated behavior. Online distillation mitigates this drift by training on model-generated rollouts, yet uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it can under-emphasize reference-supported rollout tokens and supervise omitted facts only indirectly. We introduce RoCo-ACE, a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection. RoCo uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens, while ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation. Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy among compared methods while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

</details>


### [172] [RSMeM: Knowledge-Enhanced Memory Evolution for Remote Sensing Agents with Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24772)
*Bingxian Wu,Yu Zhang,Zonghao Guo,Tang Liu,Chen Qian,Yuxiang Lu,Xingbo Du,Yanghao Li,Yidan Zhang,Chi Chen,Ling Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSMeM是一种面向遥感领域的知识增强记忆演化机制，通过分层知识检索和失败经验提炼，提升大语言模型在多步工具执行任务中的鲁棒性和知识复用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用大语言模型的遥感智能体存在领域知识匮乏、工作流程脆弱易错，且失败经验无法被有效复用的问题。

Method: 提出RSMeM机制，包含两个核心组件：1）分层知识基础（Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding），通过层级化领域语料库的感知检索引导规划和工具选择；2）失败感知经验精炼（Failure-Aware Experience Refinement），将标注失败的工具执行轨迹蒸馏为可复用约束，迭代优化下一轮工具执行。

Result: 在EarthBench数据集上，RSMeM显著提升了多种大语言模型骨干的工具使用性能和端到端问答准确率。其中DeepSeek-V3.2提升6%准确率，仅增加不到1%的额外经验token。

Conclusion: RSMeM验证了领域知识注入与失败经验迭代蒸馏可有效增强遥感智能体的鲁棒性和工具执行能力，且知识密度高、开销低。

Abstract: Geoscience research requires complex analysis and domain expertise, with remote sensing (RS) observations as a key foundation. However, existing RS agents built on general-purpose LLMs remain largely domain-agnostic, resulting in brittle and error-prone workflows. Moreover, these failures are seldom consolidated into a reusable experience for subsequent analyses. To address this issue, we introduce RSMeM, a knowledge-enhanced memory evolution mechanism that bootstraps RS agents with pre-distilled domain knowledge and iteratively integrates online experience for robust multi-step tool execution. RSMeM is composed of two components: (i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding, which performs taxonomy-aware retrieval over a hierarchical domain corpus to guide planning and tool selection; and (ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement, which distills failure-annotated tool-use traces into reusable constraints for next-round tool execution. By iteratively employing these two processes, RS agents can evolve to absorb task-level domain knowledge and effectively translate it into instance-level execution experience. Extensive experiments on EarthBench demonstrate that RSMeM consistently improves tool-use performance and end-to-end answer across a diverse set of LLM backbones. Notably, RSMeM achieves a 6% accuracy improvement on DeepSeek-V3.2 with less than 1% additional experience tokens, demonstrating the strong knowledge density of our distilled experience. Our code is available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/RSMeM

</details>


### [173] [Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR): Cloud Workload Conformal Prediction for Virtual Machines in Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24773)
*Mehryar Majd,Feng Cheng,Ali Pahlevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自助法共形预测（Bootstrapping Conformal Prediction）的数据驱动区间预测方法，用于云环境中虚拟机（VM）的右规模推荐（Right-sizing Recommendations, RSR），旨在提高大规模云基础设施的资源利用效率和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟机分配与调度方法未能有效应对云资源利用率的波动性和不可预测性，导致资源过度配置或配置不足，造成成本浪费与性能下降。

Method: 采用自助法共形预测（Bootstrapping Conformal Prediction）构建预测区间（Prediction Intervals），结合机器学习回归技术与回测（backtesting）评估，学习负载利用模式、识别多时间序列相关性，并预测中长期利用趋势。

Result: AI驱动的回归模型在云资源利用预测中取得了有前景的结果，通过模型排名识别出适用于长期虚拟机候选方案的最佳方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够增强右规模推荐能力，支持在动态云环境中实现更具成本效益的资源分配。

Abstract: Managing cloud infrastructure efficiently, especially in environments of large cloud providers or hyperscalers, requires optimizing the use of physical resources to minimize costs and maximize performance. Selecting the right virtual machine (VM) sizes is crucial to achieving cost efficiency in these dynamic environments. However, traditional VM allocation and scheduling approaches often fail to account for the fluctuating and unpredictable nature of VM utilization, leading to inefficiencies such as over- or under-provisioning of resources. High-quality interval prediction helps accurately capture uncertainty in cloud resource demand and supports cloud operators in efficient instance provisioning.
  As an effective and reliable framework for constructing prediction intervals (PIs), conformal prediction (CP) is used for mid- and long-term forecasting tasks in cloud computing environments. This study proposes a new data-driven PI construction approach using bootstrapping conformal prediction for modern, dynamic, data-driven Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR) to enhance provisioning for diverse application workloads on hyperscalers. By learning workload utilization patterns, identifying correlations across multiple time series, and predicting medium- to long-term utilization trends, this research seeks to improve the efficiency of cloud and data center operations through an AI/ML-based provisioning pipeline.
  Our study demonstrates that AI-driven models, powered by machine learning regression techniques and evaluated using backtesting, achieve promising forecasting results for cloud resource utilization. Additionally, we rank the selected models to identify top-performing approaches for long-life VM candidates. The proposed framework enhances right-sizing recommendations and supports more cost-effective resource allocation in dynamic cloud environments.

</details>


### [174] [Atmospheric Diffusion-Guided Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Nuclear Radiation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24774)
*Tengfei Lyu,Jindong Han,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NRFormer+模型，结合非平稳时间注意力、密度自适应空间注意力和大气扩散模块，解决核辐射预测中的非平稳性、站点分布不均和气象耦合三大难题，在13个基线上取得最佳性能，突发变化MAE降低19.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 福岛事故和处理水排放加剧了核辐射风险，现有监测网络虽数据丰富，但难以转化为可靠预测，主要面临非平稳时序、站点空间分布极度不均、辐射与气象异质因素协同演化三大挑战。

Method: 提出NRFormer+时空Transformer架构，包含三部分：非平稳时间注意力处理时序非平稳性；密度自适应空间注意力解决站点分布不均；大气扩散模块模拟气象驱动的辐射扩散过程，作为物理先验嵌入网络。

Result: 在两个数据集上超越全部13个基线模型，突发变化MAE相比最强基线降低19.1%，推理延迟相当。

Conclusion: NRFormer+有效融合物理知识与深度学习，为全国范围核辐射预测提供了新范式，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Nuclear radiation, the energy released during atomic decay, poses persistent risks to public health and the environment, and concerns have only grown since the Fukushima accident and the recent commencement of treated-water discharge. Modern monitoring networks now record radiation levels and accompanying weather conditions at thousands of stations, opening the door to nationwide forecasting that can inform emergency response, agricultural advisories, and routine public-safety decisions. However, turning this abundance of monitoring data into reliable forecasts is difficult for three reasons. First, the time series at each station are highly non-stationary, shaped by radioactive decay, weather variability, and irregular human interventions. Second, monitoring stations are severely unevenly distributed in space. Roughly 78% of Japan's stations sit in less than 6% of the country, clustered near Fukushima, which breaks the assumptions of standard graph-based models. Third, radiation co-evolves with heterogeneous context such as wind, temperature, and humidity through atmospheric transport processes that purely data-driven models struggle to capture from observations alone. In this study, we introduce NRFormer+, a spatio-temporal Transformer for nationwide nuclear radiation forecasting. NRFormer+ couples non-stationary temporal attention and density-adaptive spatial attention with a new atmospheric diffusion module that estimates how meteorology drives radiation dispersion and injects this physical signal into the network as an architectural prior. NRFormer+ delivers state-of-the-art accuracy on both datasets across all 13 baselines, reducing sudden-change MAE by up to 19.1% over the strongest baseline at comparable inference latency. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/tfeilyu/NRFormer_Plus.

</details>


### [175] [Steering topology distributions for unified generative design of architected metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24777)
*Haolin Li,Yuyang Miao,Menglei Li,Jinshuai Bai,Liyuan Wang,Xin Liu,Bo Gao,Jiantao Liu,Danilo Mandic,Zahra Sharif Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi,Weiqiu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenTO框架，通过预训练的扩散模型生成拓扑先验，并针对不同物理设计任务进行迭代优化，实现了可重用的拓扑知识驱动的高性能超材料设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑设计方法通常针对单个设计问题定制，难以在目标、约束和物理功能变化时有效复用拓扑知识，缺乏统一且广泛适用的设计框架。

Method: GenTO首先在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型，学习拓扑先验分布；随后通过用户定义的物理目标和约束，迭代引导该分布向任务特定的高性能区域偏移，实现从优化单个结构到优化任务适配的拓扑分布的转变。

Result: 在热学极值化、多目标形态控制、特定性能的拉胀设计、振动传输设计等多个拓扑设计问题中，GenTO复用了预训练的拓扑先验，保持了结构多样性，达到了高性能解，并通过数值基准和实验验证得到支持。

Conclusion: 可重用的拓扑知识可作为统一原则，实现有效且可扩展的超材料设计。

Abstract: Architected metamaterials derive their functions from structure, creating vast opportunities to program physical responses through topology design. However, existing design methods are often tailored to individual design problems, making limited use of topology knowledge for effective and broadly applicable design as objectives, constraints, and physical functions change. Here we introduce Generative Topology Optimization (GenTO), a unified framework that turns a learned topology prior into a reusable design engine. GenTO trains a diffusion model on a large full-order topology dataset and then iteratively steers the resulting topology distribution toward task-specific high-performing regions using user-defined physical objectives and constraints. This shifts the object of optimization from a single structure to a task-adapted topology distribution. Across topology design problems spanning thermal extremization, multi-objective morphology control, property-targeted auxetic design, and vibration transmission design, GenTO reuses pretrained topology priors for heterogeneous tasks, preserves structural diversity, and reaches high-performing solutions supported by numerical benchmarks and experimental validation. These results establish reusable topology knowledge as a unified principle for effective and scalable architected metamaterial design.

</details>


### [176] [HOBA: Hierarchical On-Policy Bidding Agents for Adaptive Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24779)
*Ji Wu,Yunshan Peng,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Wenzheng Shu,Jinan Pang,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HOBA分层强化学习框架，将在线广告竞价中的策略推理、模型选择和出价执行解耦到三个时间尺度，通过LLM推理超参数、SARSA选择专家模型、动态专家池执行出价，在AuctionNet和在线A/B测试中取得显著提升，目标成本提高3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线广告竞价系统依赖离线训练的多专家模型，存在两大局限性：缺乏对非平稳拍卖市场的在线适应能力，以及依赖昂贵的手动超参数调优。

Method: 提出HOBA分层强化学习框架：高层LLM通过Think-Act-Observe-Reflect循环推断超参数；中层SARSA代理结合因果调整消除选择偏差，动态选择专家模型；低层动态专家池（PID、MPC、IQL、Decision Transformer）在高层约束下执行出价。在线学习局限于离散的专家选择而非连续出价优化。

Result: 在AuctionNet基准和在线A/B测试中均优于基线方法，在线部署中目标成本提升3.6%。

Conclusion: HOBA通过分层多智能体竞价范式，在降低探索风险的同时保持适应性，证明了其层次化框架在在线广告竞价中的有效性。

Abstract: Online advertising bidding systems typically deploy multiple offline-trained expert models (e.g., PID controllers, model predictive control, offline RL policies) but face two critical limitations: lack of online adaptability to non-stationary auction markets, and reliance on costly manual tuning of hyperparameters such as bid bounds and budget pacing constraints. We propose HOBA (Hierarchical On-policy Bidding Agents), a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that decouples strategic reasoning, model selection, and bid execution across three time scales. At the high level, a large language model infers hyperparameters from contextual signals through a Think-Act-Observe-Reflect loop with historical experience retrieval. At the mid level, a SARSA agent dynamically selects among expert models, incorporating causal adjustment to eliminate selection bias. At the low level, a dynamic expert pool (PID, MPC, IQL, Decision Transformer) executes bids under high-level constraints. This design confines online learning to discrete expert selection rather than continuous bid optimization, significantly reducing exploration risk while maintaining adaptability. Experiments on the AuctionNet benchmark and a large-scale A/B test demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. In a large-scale online deployment, HOBA delivered substantial business value, achieving a +3.6\% increase in target cost, proving the effectiveness of our hierarchical multi-agent bidding paradigm.

</details>


### [177] [LivingArena: Do LLMs Know What Other LLMs Don't? Peer-Probing as Scalable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24780)
*Xingyu Chen,Rui Wang,Zhaopeng Tu,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LivingArena框架，利用LLM互问互答自动评估，通过对抗性提问挖掘模型知识边界，生成抗污染的排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准存在数据污染和饱和问题，人类偏好主观性强，需要一种自动化、抗污染、可扩展的评估方法。

Method: 模型轮流充当提问者和回答者，提问者通过挖掘对手知识盲区获得奖励，由强模型陪审团验证问题客观可答。

Result: 在10个前沿LLM上生成稳定的Elo排行榜，模型能识别并利用对手的认知边界，自对弈中不断攻击对手薄弱维度。

Conclusion: Peer probing评估的事实严谨性和高阶探测能力与人类偏好弱相关，提供了一种可扩展、低成本的连续评估方案。

Abstract: Evaluating frontier LLMs is challenging: static benchmarks suffer from contamination and saturation -- leaving users unable to distinguish top models and developers blind to specific failure modes -- while human preference is subjective. In this paper, our question is: \emph{Do LLMs know what other LLMs don't? And can we leverage this dynamic for evaluation?} We present \textbf{LivingArena}, an automated, contamination-resistant evaluation framework. In this framework, models take turns proposing questions, aiming to pose items that opponents cannot answer correctly. Questioners are encouraged to actively identify and exploit opponents' knowledge boundaries, receiving rewards when the answerer fails, while the answerer is rewarded otherwise. To ensure questions contain objectively verifiable answers, a judge panel of strong models validates them, penalizing questioners if validation fails. Evaluating ten frontier LLMs, LivingArena yields a stable Elo leaderboard. Our behavioral analyses show that models identify and exploit their peers' cognitive boundaries: self-play and tournament logs indicate that they localize and double down on opponents' weak dimensions. Beyond static knowledge recall, peer probing measures factual rigor and the higher-order ability to probe an opponent's weaknesses, correlating only weakly with human preference and offering a scalable, low-cost approach to continuous evaluation.

</details>


### [178] [Personalization, Personas, and Forecasting in Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24782)
*James Wedgwood,Pratiksha Thaker,Neil Kale,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了LLM在不同提示框架（用户适配、角色扮演、第三人称预测）下的文化价值对齐效果。基于WVS调查，评估了GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen3-235B四个模型，发现提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素：第三人称预测对齐效果最好，个性化与角色扮演较弱，且对齐优势集中在宗教观、性别角色等显性价值维度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM行为是否受人类身份提示框架（用户适配、角色扮演、第三人称预测）的影响，以及这些框架在文化价值对齐上是否可互换。

Method: 使用世界价值观调查（WVS）的101道问题，覆盖13种语言-国家组合，测试GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen3-235B四个模型。比较语言基线、用户国家提示、角色国家提示、第三人称提示四种框架，共收集21,008条模型响应。

Result: 提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素。第三人称预测在四个模型中的三个上取得最佳方向性对齐；个性化和角色扮演效果较弱或不稳定。对齐改进集中在宗教观、性别角色、工作物质主义等显性维度，而制度信任和民主相关问题仍难以对齐。

Conclusion: 提示框架不是文化价值诱导中的装饰性选择，它会显著改变模型行为和测量到的对齐程度。

Abstract: LLM behavior may be conditioned by human identity in several ways: they may be asked to adapt to users, role-play populations, or forecast how people would answer value-laden questions. We test whether these framings are interchangeable using the World Values Survey (WVS). We evaluate GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Qwen3-235B on 101 WVS-derived questions across 13 language-country slices, comparing a language-only baseline with user-country, persona-country, and third-person prompts. Across 21,008 model-response rows, prompt framing is a first-order determinant of cultural alignment: country cues often shift answers substantially, but not all shifts move toward matched human response distributions. Third-person forecasting yields the strongest directional alignment for three of the four hosted models, while personalization and role-play are weaker or less stable. Alignment gains concentrate on salient value dimensions such as religiosity, gender roles, and work-oriented material values, whereas institutional trust and democracy-related questions remain difficult. These results show that prompt framing is not a cosmetic choice in cultural value elicitation; it changes both model behavior and measured alignment.

</details>


### [179] [Unified Semantic Modeling Framework for Large-Scale Job Understanding at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24783)
*Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Jianqiang Shen,Qi Xiao,Benjamin Hoan Le,Wen Pu,Saurabh Gupta,Ran Zhou,Neha Saraf,Alice Leung,Qianqi Shen,Liangjie Hong,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的统一语义建模框架，通过合成任务微调和多适配器架构，实现了对非结构化招聘信息的零样本理解，在提升性能的同时降低了运营复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn需要将非结构化的招聘信息转化为标准化属性，以支持多项产品，但现有系统在可扩展性、成本效益和性能方面面临挑战。

Method: 1. 基于开源SLM进行微调，使用精心设计的合成任务（涵盖分类和实体抽取）并增强推理轨迹；2. 引入多适配器架构，通过属性分组实现高效的任务特定适配和模型管理。

Result: 离线评估和在线A/B测试均显示，该方法在显著提升性能的同时降低了运营复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建工业级文本理解系统提供了实用见解，证明了SLM结合合成任务微调与多适配器架构的有效性。

Abstract: Job understanding is critical to LinkedIn's mission of connecting talent with opportunity. This task involves transforming unstructured and noisy job postings into standardized or derived job attributes that power numerous LinkedIn products. However, building a scalable, cost-efficient, and high-performing job understanding system remains challenging. In this paper, we present a unified semantic modeling framework powered by a small language model (SLM) to address the challenges. We begin by fine-tuning an open-source SLM using a suite of carefully curated synthetic tasks augmented with reasoning traces. These tasks jointly target taxonomy-guided classification and taxonomy-agnostic entity extraction. This allows the resulting model to acquire robust zero-shot generalization for job understanding in structured and unstructured contexts. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a multi-adapter architecture with attribute grouping to facilitate efficient task-specific adaptation while streamlining model management across diverse downstream attributes. Offline evaluations and online A/B tests demonstrate significant performance improvement while reducing operational complexity. Our work provides practical insights into building industry-scale text understanding systems.

</details>


### [180] [On the Use of LLMs for Specialised Terminology: A Good Alternative to Corpora?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24784)
*Joachim Minder,Guillaume Wisniewski,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种大型语言模型（GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5和DeepSeek）在英法专业翻译中术语等价词查找的能力。实验涵盖地球环境与行星科学（EEPS）和自然语言处理（NLP）两个领域，基于每领域80个术语，对比术语模式和翻译模式两种提示策略。结果表明，Claude Sonnet 4.5在最优配置下表现最佳，DeepSeek稳定性最强，但所有模型目前仍无法替代专业语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业翻译依赖语料库等术语资源，但这些资源的编制和利用存在耗时、需技术技能、数据难获取等局限。本研究旨在探索LLM能否帮助专业译员克服这些困难，快速找到英法术语等价词。

Method: 选取GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5和DeepSeek四种专有模型，在地球环境与行星科学（EEPS）和自然语言处理（NLP）两个领域中，各基于80个术语进行实验。比较两种提示策略：术语模式和翻译模式，并分析模型的置信度估计。

Result: 模型、提示策略和领域之间存在明显差异。Claude Sonnet 4.5在最优配置下表现最好，DeepSeek稳定性最强。置信度估计仅能部分反映术语准确性。

Conclusion: LLM可成为专业译员的有用工具，但现阶段尚无法取代专业语料库。研究为未来在工作和教育场景中探索LLM对专业译员的实际实用性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Specialised translation relies on the use of documentary and terminological resources, including corpora. These resources are particularly useful for terminology. However, their compilation and exploitation have several limitations: they require time, technical skills and access to data that can be difficult to collect. This study examines the extent to which LLMs can assist specialised translators in finding equivalents from English to French. We evaluate four proprietary models, GPT-4o, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and DeepSeek, in two specialised domains, Earth, Environmental and Planetary Sciences (EEPS) and Natural Language Processing (NLP). The experiment is based on 80 terms per domain and compares two prompting strategies: a terminology and a translation mode. The results highlight clear differences between models, prompting strategies and, to a lesser extent, domains. Claude Sonnet 4.5 achieves the best results in the most favourable configuration, while DeepSeek stands out for its greater stability. Analysis of confidence estimates also shows that they are only a partial indicator of terminological accuracy. Overall, the findings suggest that LLMs can be useful tools for specialised translators, but cannot, at this stage, replace specialised corpora. This research therefore paves the way for future work on the real practical usefulness of LLMs for specialised translators in work and educational contexts.

</details>


### [181] [SpecPrefetch: Parameter-Efficient Expert Prefetching for Sparse MoE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24787)
*Jinwei Kong,Runqi Meng,Fanyi Wang,Wentao Qiu,Haotian Hu,Yongjian Zhou,Zhenhua Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecPrefetch，一种参数高效预取框架，通过轻量适配器预测下一层专家候选并异步传输，解决MoE模型推理中专家卸载的路由依赖瓶颈，在减少可训练参数的同时实现最高20%的解码吞吐提升。
