These skews reproduce across three re-partitions -- disjoint player-account sets (primary), time, and disjoint rating bands -- and on an out-of-sample month eight months later. On the primary split, each position's skew is measured once in each account group, and the replication slope asks how well one measurement predicts the other after removing rating and opening-family effects: one means undiminished carry-over; zero, no linear relation. We find 0.69 (family-clustered 95% CI [0.65, 0.74]), rising to 0.94 on the most-popular, best-measured positions. The slope's value depends on the position mix. Existence is the invariant claim: it survives every tighter evaluation band, search depth, calibration, and popularity cutoff we test, and replicates within blitz and rapid separately. The typical skew is small (median $|δ| \approx 0.018$, about two percentage points of White score), yet it reproduces, position by position, across disjoint accounts. At these positions the disfavoured side also thinks longer. Even where the evaluation is most confident, it is not a sufficient statistic for human outcomes. The result is observational, and the causal question is left to a pre-registered randomised companion study.

</details>


### [251] [OrchBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Plans in Isolation via Deterministic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25656)
*Zhenzhen Ren,Jiyan He,Xinpeng Zhang,Zhenxing Qian,Ke Han,Shuxin Zheng,GuoBiao Li,Xiaoqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchBench是一个基于仿真的多智能体编排计划评估基准，通过有向无环图（DAG）建模任务依赖，在不调用真实工作智能体的情况下评估编排计划质量，仅需1.3%的token和10.3%的时间即可达到与真实执行高度相关（r=0.816）的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端评估方式将编排计划质量与工作智能体能力、工具可靠性及环境噪声混杂在一起，且时间和token成本随工作流规模快速增长，缺乏一种高效、可解释的多智能体编排计划隔离评估方法。

Method: 从真实任务构建可控大小和并行度的DAG，给定DAG、智能体上下文限制和预算，规划器分配子任务并指定智能体间信息传输及保留比例，通过确定性模拟器在不调用工作智能体的情况下评估计划质量、完工时间和token成本。

Result: OrchBench模拟评分与Claude Code真实执行评分皮尔逊相关系数r=0.816，仅需1.3%的token和10.3%的耗时。发现保留任务关键信息比增加智能体数量更重要，并行性收益会因协调失败累积而递减。

Conclusion: OrchBench是一个高效且可解释的基准，可用于比较和诊断多智能体编排计划。

Abstract: Complex tasks often decompose into parallelizable yet interdependent subtasks, making orchestration critical to the performance of multi-agent systems (MAS). Existing evaluations typically rely on end-to-end execution, which conflates orchestration-plan quality with worker capabilities, tool reliability, and environmental noise. Moreover, the time and token costs of real execution grow rapidly with workflow scale, making systematic evaluation expensive. We present OrchBench, a simulation-based benchmark for evaluating multi-agent orchestration plans in isolation. Starting from real-world tasks, OrchBench constructs directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that encode task dependencies, with controlled sizes and degrees of parallelism. Given a DAG, a per-agent context limit, and an agent budget, the evaluated planner assigns subtasks to agents and specifies cross-agent information transfers and their retention ratios. A deterministic simulator evaluates the resulting plan without invoking worker agents and returns interpretable measures of result quality, makespan, and token cost. The simulated scores produced by OrchBench correlate strongly with quality scores from Claude Code executions, achieving a Pearson correlation of \(r=0.816\), while requiring only \(1.3\%\) of the tokens and \(10.3\%\) of the wall-clock time. Across diverse planners and workflow scales, we find that preserving task-critical information is more important than simply increasing the number of agents, and the benefits of parallelism diminish as coordination failures accumulate. These results establish OrchBench as an efficient and interpretable benchmark for comparing and diagnosing multi-agent orchestration plans.

</details>


### [252] [CoRT: Counterfactual Replay for Token-Level Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25659)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Wen Wang,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoRT是一种基于反事实重放的token级信用分配方法，通过对比有/无rubric条件下的token对数似然差异来生成权重，在不引入额外评分模型的情况下提升GRPO训练效果，平均提高4.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRPO风格管道中，rubric-based强化学习将结构化判断简化为标量响应级奖励，并均匀广播到所有token，缺乏对响应内部不同token的信用分配机制。

Method: CoRT使用反事实重放，在相同采样响应下对比原始rubric条件提示和无标准提示，计算token级对数似然差异作为对rubric上下文依赖的代理，映射为有界归一化权重，用于重新分配GRPO优势值。

Result: 在绝大多数对比中CoRT优于匹配的响应级GRPO，平均增益4.4个百分点，与学习的token级信用基线竞争但无需单独的相关性学习阶段。

Conclusion: 策略内部的反事实似然对比为响应内信用分配提供了有效的训练信号，同时保留了GRPO的简洁性和稳定性。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning enriches language model training by evaluating model outputs against explicit criteria. Yet in GRPO-style pipelines, these structured judgments are reduced to a scalar response-level reward and converted into a response-level advantage, which is broadcast uniformly to all generated tokens. This leaves no explicit mechanism for allocating credit within a response, even when different criteria are grounded in different spans, formatting decisions, or semantic choices. We propose CoRT, a token-level credit weighting method for rubric-conditioned GRPO. Instead of training an auxiliary token scoring model, CoRT uses counterfactual replay to rescore the same sampled response under the original rubric-conditioned prompt and a matched criteria-free prompt. The resulting tokenwise log-likelihood contrasts serve as a proxy for dependence on the rubric context. CoRT maps these contrasts to bounded, response-normalized weights and uses them to redistribute the signed GRPO advantage across tokens, without introducing an auxiliary scorer or changing the response-level reward. Experiments across instruction-tuned models and reward granularities show that CoRT improves over matched response-level GRPO in the vast majority of comparisons, with an average gain of 4.4 percentage points. The method remains competitive with learned token-level credit baselines while avoiding a separate relevance-learning stage. These results suggest that policy-internal counterfactual likelihood contrasts provide an effective training signal for within-response credit allocation while retaining the simplicity and stability of GRPO.

</details>


### [253] [Desktop-Delta Bench: Do Computer-Use Models Understand Desktop GUI Transitions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26041)
*Abhishek Pillai,Samir Kumar Nayak,Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)，一个用于评估桌面计算机使用代理（CUA）在动作执行后是否能够正确重建因果、任务相关状态变化的离线步骤级基准。包含2,013个人工验证实例，覆盖~15个应用和50个任务域，通过时序排序和前后状态对比两项任务，揭示了现有模型在状态验证、源追踪和上下文感知控制方面的系统性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要度量最终任务成功或单帧定位，无法隔离评估模型是否能重建动作产生的因果、任务相关的状态变化——这对于拒绝过时观测、验证进度和从失败中恢复至关重要。由于推理、远程输入、应用渲染和截图捕获的异步性，下一观测可能延迟、遮挡、瞬态或无关，容易被误读为进度并带入后续规划。

Method: 构建了Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB)，包含2,013个人工验证实例，来自新颖的多应用Linux轨迹（约15个应用、50个任务域）。设置两项互补任务：463个3帧时序排序实例（含105个跨轨迹干扰项）和1,550个标注了5种动作及其负载的前后状态对。评估了8个模型家族（32种排序设置和16种单动作设置）。

Result: 排序任务未饱和：最佳非干扰和干扰精确匹配率分别为65.1%和65.7%。任务上下文使干扰识别提升6.9个百分点但非干扰精确匹配下降2.2个百分点；错误分析显示模型系统性复制呈现的A-B-C顺序。单动作结果：推断动作类别比定位更难（点击F1=0.96 vs 拖拽0.76），但识别的拖拽通常定位良好。

Conclusion: DDB通过填补GUI定位和最终任务成功之间的缺失诊断层，补充了端到端基准，能够有针对性地改进桌面CUA的验证、可靠性和恢复能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act through desktop GUIs to complete long-horizon tasks. Current benchmarks primarily measure end-task success or single-frame grounding. Neither isolates whether a model can reconstruct the causal, task-relevant transition produced by an action- crucial for rejecting stale observations, verifying progress, and recovering from failure. This is difficult because inference, remote input, app rendering, and screenshot capture are asynchronous: the next observation may be delayed, occluded, transient, or unrelated, then misread as progress and carried into subsequent planning. We introduce Desktop-Delta Bench (DDB), an offline step-level benchmark with 2,013 human-verified instances from novel, multi-app Linux trajectories across ~15 applications and 50 task domains. DDB trajectories targets 3 failure dimensions- state verification, source tracking, and context-aware control- through 2 complementary tasks: 463 3-frame temporal-ordering instances, including 105 with a cross-trajectory decoy, and 1,550 before-after pairs labeled from 5 actions + its payload. We evaluate 8 closed and open-source model families across 32 ordering and 16 single-action settings, observing consistent gaps. Ordering remains unsaturated: best non-decoy and decoy exact-match rates are 65.1% and 65.7%. Task context improves decoy identification by 6.9 percentage points but reduces non-decoy exact match by 2.2 points; error analysis reveals systematic copying of the presented A-B-C order. Single-action results show that inferring the action family is harder than locating it: click F1 is 0.96 vs, 0.76 for drag, while recognized drags are generally localized well. DDB, thus, complements end-to-end benchmarks by filling the missing diagnostic layer between GUI grounding and final task success, enabling targeted improvements to desktop CUA verification, reliability, and recovery.

</details>


### [254] [OmniDelta: Skill-Driven Budget Allocation for Token Compression in OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25669)
*Haoyang Huang,Wenjie Huang,Tianqi Xu,Hongyaoxing Gu,Kang Tan,Yikai Fu,Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Baolin Zhang,Jun Zhang,Xinyi Hu,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingchen Wang,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmniDelta框架，通过技能驱动的感知分配策略优化跨模态预算分配，在保持高精度的同时显著降低多模态大模型的内存和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩方法聚焦于固定预算下的Token选择，但忽略了跨模态间预算分配问题，导致关键证据丢失或冗余保留。

Method: OmniDelta构建音频和视频技能池，通过意图感知的模态间分配和内容感知的模态内分配，结合局部复杂度和时间冗余重新分配预算。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，25% Token保留率下GPU内存减少22%，端到端速度提升1.64倍，在准确率-效率Pareto前沿上取得新突破。

Conclusion: OmniDelta是一种无需训练、即插即用的高效压缩框架，有效解决了多模态大模型的长序列推理瓶颈。

Abstract: Emerging Omni-modal Large Language Models (OmniLLMs) enable unified understanding of text, audio, and video, but their long audio-video token sequences introduce substantial memory and inference costs. Existing compression methods mainly focus on selecting important tokens under fixed budgets, leaving the preceding budget-allocation problem underexplored. We show that direct query-to-audio/video similarity is unreliable for inter-modal budget allocation, and that uniform intra-modal budgets can miss key evidence while retaining redundant content. To address these limitations, we propose OmniDelta, a training-free, skill-driven framework that couples intent-aware inter-modal allocation with content-aware intra-modal allocation. OmniDelta first constructs audio and video skill pools to shift the fixed retained-token budget according to query demand, then reallocates modality budgets over audio segments and video frames using local complexity and temporal redundancy. The resulting local budgets can be combined with existing pruning strategies, preserving the total retained-token ratio while changing where the budget is spent. Experiments on four audio-video benchmarks with two Qwen2.5-Omni models show that OmniDelta establishes a new accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across pruning ratios. At 25% token retention on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniDelta reduces GPU memory by 22.0% and achieves a 1.64x end-to-end speedup over full-token inference.

</details>


### [255] [DecoEvo: Score-Decoupled Co-Evolution of Solver and Rubric-Generator Skills in Text Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25675)
*Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Junlin Liu,Shuaiyu Zhou,Haiyan Wu,Haihan Shi,Chenxi Zhou,Hanqing Li,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecoEvo通过解耦式协同进化策略，将求解器技能和评估标准生成器技能分开优化，在无需人工标注评估标准的情况下，显著提升了LLM在开放任务上的表现，在5个基准测试中平均相对提升2.8-5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本空间优化方法在开放任务中面临瓶颈：当求解器在评估指标上改进后，未覆盖的评价维度会失去优化信号；而直接用当前求解器分数更新评估标准，又可能因分数提升仅来自标准变简单而产生虚假进步。

Method: DecoEvo提出解耦式协同进化：求解器技能通过准则级反馈更新；评估标准生成器技能通过独立的审计（需求覆盖率和响应区分度）来更新，与求解器总分无关，从而聚焦于求解器新暴露出的弱点。

Result: 在5个基准测试和3种LLM骨干网络上，DecoEvo在所有指标上均优于对比方法，相比SkillOpt平均提升2.8-5.0%。

Conclusion: 解耦评估标准与求解器的优化目标，有效避免了虚假进步，持续暴露求解器新弱点，提升了开放任务优化效果。

Abstract: Text-space optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) by editing external natural-language artifacts rather than model weights, so the optimized artifacts remain inspectable and the model can be treated as a black box. However, most existing text-space methods keep evaluation fixed. On open-ended tasks, this can become a bottleneck: once the solver improves on the criteria a rubric measures, omitted dimensions remain invisible to the optimization signal. Simply evolving the rubric is also unreliable when updates are selected by the current solver's score, because apparent progress can come from making the rubric easier to satisfy. We introduce DecoEvo (Decoupled Co-Evolution), which co-evolves a solver skill and a rubric-generator skill under decoupled objectives without using gold rubrics during optimization. The solver skill is updated using criterion-level feedback, while the rubric-generator skill is revised through complementary audits of requirement coverage and response discrimination that are independent of aggregate solver score. This separation focuses generator updates on newly exposed solver weaknesses, reducing repeated emphasis on criteria the solver already satisfies. Under each benchmark's official evaluation, DecoEvo outperforms all compared methods across five benchmarks and three LLM backbones, yielding 2.8--5.0\% relative gains over SkillOpt in the five-benchmark average.

</details>


### [256] [Cognivia: A Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Copilot for Evidence-Based Mental Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25681)
*Qi Chen,Siria Xiyueyao Luo,Jian Wang,Yuan Shi,Haocong Rao,Xuejiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognivia，一个基于证据的AI治疗师，通过整合自动认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成，在权威CBT文本和专家监督下微调轻量级LLM，并首次提出层级质量评估框架，实验表明其在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知扭曲会加剧负面情绪并导致心理健康障碍，CBT虽有效但受限于专业治疗师短缺；现有LLM方法存在领域特异性不足、回应过于谄媚以及缺乏认知扭曲标注等问题。

Method: 基于权威CBT文本和心理健康问答数据构建增强数据集，采用多阶段提示和结构化生成策略，在行为科学专家监督下微调轻量级LLM获得Cognivia；并提出首个用于评估LLM生成理性回应的层级质量评估框架。

Result: Cognivia在词汇指标、基于LLM的评判和10位行为科学专家的人工评估中，在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成上均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Cognivia在认知扭曲识别和理性回应生成上表现出有效性，为大规模CBT应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cognitive distortion amplifies negative emotions and contributes to mental health disorders. Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) is an effective way to address cognitive distortions, but its large-scale application is limited by the shortage of professional therapists. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently been explored for mental health applications, existing methods still suffer from limited domain specificity, overly flattering responses, and the absence of well-defined annotations for cognitive distortions. This paper proposes Cognivia, an evidence-based artificial intelligence therapist that integrates automatic cognitive distortion identification and rational response generation. Our framework is built on authoritative CBT texts widely regarded as core paradigms and standard references. It is further augmented with mental health question-answer (Q and A) data, and employs multi-stage prompting and structured generation strategies under the supervision of behavioral science experts. Then we fine-tune a lightweight LLM on this augmented CBT dataset to obtain Cognivia. In addition, we propose the first hierarchical quality evaluation framework for assessing LLM-generated rational responses, developed through collaboration between AI researchers and behavioral science experts. Cognivia is evaluated using lexical metrics, LLM-based Judges with two complementary criteria, and human evaluation by 10 behavioral science experts. It consistently outperforms the baseline methods in cognitive distortion recognition and rational response generation, demonstrating its effectiveness. Our code is available at https://github.com/SNOWTEAM2023/Cognivia.

</details>


### [257] [Nudging Sustainable Choices through LLM-Generated Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25726)
*Haya Halimeh,Dietmar Jannach,Oliver Müller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过生成式AI生成基于助推理论的可解释性信息，发现框架效应和描述性社会规范能显著提升用户对可持续产品的选择，而单纯披露可持续信息虽能提升解释评价却无法改变实际选择行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统影响用户日常消费，有望促进可持续选择。已有研究证明解释信息能影响用户对推荐的感知并支持更明智的决策。本文主张解释信息还可通过突出可持续信息作为行为助推手段，但尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 利用生成式AI基于助推理论生成可持续感知的解释，通过人类评估和LLM评判进行验证；在低参与度（速溶咖啡）和高参与度（酒店预订）两个领域进行两项随机实验（N=529），让用户在偏好匹配的推荐中选择。

Result: 两个领域一致显示：简单披露可持续信息不改变选择；框架化呈现或引用描述性社会规范显著增加可持续选择并降低决策难度。感知与行为出现分离——单纯披露改善了解释评价但未转化为可持续选择行为。

Conclusion: 证明了LLM可大规模生成理论支撑的解释，指向可操作的基于解释的社会公益干预方案；讨论了生成式AI在自适应解释设计中的应用前景。

Abstract: Recommender systems mediate everyday consumption, offering a promising channel for encouraging sustainable choices. Prior research shows that explanations influence users' perceptions of recommendations and can support more informed decisions. We argue that explanations can also serve as behavioral nudges by foregrounding sustainability information at the moment of choice. This study investigates how different behavioral framings of sustainability information in recommendation explanations affect user choices and perceptions. Using generative AI, we generate sustainability-aware explanations by drawing on nudge theory and validate them through human evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge audits. Building on this foundation, we conduct two randomized studies ($N = 529$) in a low involvement domain (instant coffee) and a high involvement domain (hotel bookings), in which participants choose among preference matched recommendations accompanied by these explanations. Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [258] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 概念瓶颈模型通过左心室体积作为中间变量预测射血分数，发现仅用射血分数损失训练时，体积预测的分布会坍缩（因为射血分数是比例，对体积缩放不变）；额外添加体积监督虽增加少量射血分数误差，但大幅改善了体积的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证仅凭概念预测准确性是否足以判断概念瓶颈模型的有效性，以左心室体积作为射血分数预测的中间概念为例。

Method: 使用视频Transformer编码器，以左心室收缩末期和舒张末期体积作为概念层，射血分数由体积解析计算（无残差路径）；比较仅用射血分数目标训练 vs. 额外监督体积（毫升）训练。

Result: 无体积监督时，体积预测分布坍缩至0.1 mL（参考分布35.7/45.7 mL），但射血分数误差仅6.89（直接回归7.13）；体积监督将体积误差从89.8降至25.8 mL，射血分数误差增加0.4。

Conclusion: 概念准确性可能掩盖概念层缺乏物理尺度的问题；临床模型的可解释中间变量应针对训练目标的不变性结构进行验证。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [259] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自推测智能体（Self-Speculating Agent），将目标智能体模型同时用作推测器，通过联合强化学习训练，在不降低任务性能的前提下显著提升下一工具调用预测的准确率，从而减少等待延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用推测方法要么使用单独的草稿模型，要么依赖缓存轨迹，这些推测器与部署智能体的实际行为存在对齐差距（speculator-agent gap），导致推测效果不佳。作者希望缩小这一差距，并探索利用目标智能体本身作为推测器的可能性。

Method: 提出自推测智能体设计：同一模型兼具智能体模式（执行任务）和推测模式（从部分轨迹预测下一工具调用），完全复用前缀KV缓存。提出联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，从智能体自身 rollout 中推导推测目标，交替更新智能体和推测器。

Result: 在智能体搜索QA和对话式工具使用任务中，Qwen3-4B的下一工具调用Hit@1从44.1提升至61.2，Qwen3.5-4B从48.9提升至66.3，且任务成功率得以保持。

Conclusion: 自推测智能体是一种简单有效的设计，通过统一智能体与推测器并采用联合强化学习，显著提升工具调用预测精度，为减少工具调用等待延迟提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [260] [Distributed Constraint Optimization via Online Learning and Iterative Pricing with Application to Large-Scale Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25835)
*Itai Zilberstein,Pranav Rajbhandari,Steve Chien,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模分布式约束优化问题（DCOPs），从两个互补方向提出解决方案：将在线学习算法应用于DCOP并达到竞争性表现；提出分层迭代定价框架实现大规模分解。结合两者在卫星调度任务中达成99%以上请求满足率，超越基线方法的87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实世界中的DCOP实例规模过大，无法以整体方式求解，亟需可扩展的高效算法。

Method: 1) 重新审视DCOP与势博弈的联系，将现代在线学习算法适配到DCOP中；2) 提出分层分解框架：将DCOP拆分为高层元DCOP（任务分配）和独立局部优化问题（调度），并通过迭代定价机制耦合两层。

Result: 在线学习算法与代表性不完全DCOP算法竞争力相当；结合迭代定价框架，在真实卫星调度问题上满足超99%的观测请求，远超基线方法的87%。

Conclusion: 将在线学习与分层迭代定价相结合，可在大规模DCOP中实现近最优性能，为分布式卫星调度等实际应用提供有效方案。

Abstract: Distributed constraint optimization problems (DCOPs) provide a popular framework for distributed decision making under limited communication, but many real-world instances are too large to solve monolithically. We address this challenge from two complementary directions. We revisit the connection between DCOPs and potential games, and adapt modern online learning algorithms for equilibrium finding to DCOPs. We show that these algorithms are competitive with representative incomplete DCOP algorithms. We then turn to decomposition frameworks for large-scale DCOPs, motivated by large-scale decentralized satellite scheduling. We propose a new framework that separates a DCOP into two interacting subproblems: a high-level meta-DCOP for task allocation, and independent local optimization problems for scheduling. To couple the two levels, we develop a novel iterative pricing method that updates the meta-level utilities using feedback from the local optimizers. Combining our online learning methods with our iterative pricing framework, we obtain near-optimal performance on real-world decentralized satellite scheduling problem instances, fulfilling over 99% of observation requests compared with 87% for state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [261] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HiSkill，一种层次化技能图框架，将交互轨迹组织为包含技能节点、原子操作节点和类型化边的有向图，实现高层技能与可执行动作之间的桥接，在三个交互环境中超越基线并减少推理token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法通常产生扁平的文本技能集合，技能间关系未被充分利用，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 构建层次化技能图，包含技能节点、AtomicOp节点和类型化边（分解、时序转换、兼容、支持、恢复关系）；推理时检索任务相关子图，利用符号任务状态、当前技能和子图引导技能切换、AtomicOp选择和动作执行。

Result: 在三个交互环境中，HiSkill优于最先进的基线方法，同时减少了推理时的token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过层次化技能图桥接高层技能与可执行动作是有效的，能提升性能并降低推理成本。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [262] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的多智能体系统框架，利用词元级对数概率与贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播，以支持精算风险建模中的不确定性量化，在保持基准性能的同时提供工作流稳定性与不确定性分析的额外洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流是高风险决策场景，LLM的概率性输出和智能体间的依赖关系会引入不确定性，可能导致错误风险评估、不公平定价和监管不合规问题，因此需要有效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，包含数据准备、建模、审查和解释等专门智能体，在中央枢纽协调下运行。核心创新在于将词元级对数概率经长度归一化后转化为校准的任务级置信度估计，再纳入贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播。

Result: 该框架能够复现基准精算性能，同时提供对工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的额外洞察。

Conclusion: 所提出的多智能体框架在保持精算建模准确性的基础上，通过贝叶斯网络有效传播和量化不确定性，为高风险精算决策提供了更可靠的保障。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [263] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出SHarD（安全加固分发框架），通过在Pi agent框架上嵌入OS沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制，实现了可一键分发的AI编码代理安全方案，在23项OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications测试中达到100%调整得分，性能与直接配置的商业代理相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理正快速被采用，但安全风险仍是规模化部署的主要障碍。现有安全控制缺乏系统性分发机制，供应商原生方案带来生态依赖，亟需一种可分发、不依赖特定生态的安全控制方案。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法，在四种代理配置（两种商业代理有/无控制、基线框架、安全加固框架）上进行对比实验，使用23项基于OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications的测试套件。SHarD基于Pi agent框架构建，嵌入OS沙箱、技能扫描、工具限制三类控制，通过单行命令分发。

Result: SHarD调整得分100%，与最优安全配置的商业代理持平，所有测试类别无回归。发现模型非确定性会导致不一致的安全结果，自主代理行为可跨系统边界，而OS沙箱能直接缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 验证了现成安全控制可通过自定义代理框架实现规模化分发，初步提出控制框架适应性评估体系，并识别了未来研究的第三研究方向。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [264] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier是一个统一的大型语料库，包含95.7万条记录，覆盖30个基准测试、714个智能体、11,891个任务和74,205个验证器，用于标准化评估AI智能体在交互环境中的表现，揭示前沿进展的不均衡性并提供可复用的评估基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体在交互环境中的评估面临碎片化问题，不同研究使用的任务、框架、验证器和评分规则各异，导致结果难以比较。现有努力要么局限于狭窄场景，要么规模有限，或需要昂贵的重新运行，使得大量实证结果无法相互对照。

Method: 构建Messier语料库，整合公开基准测试分数，并补充五个智能体在六个未被充分代表的专业和科学领域（包括法律基准）的运行结果。每条记录按模型、框架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则标准化，并使用SOC/NAICS分类进行职业和行业分析。通过反事实重评分分析不同聚合规则的影响，并推导能力量表。

Result: 前沿进展在各基准类型上不均衡："函数调用"已饱和，"编程"进步最快，"企业工作流"最具挑战性。严格的全通过聚合规则在多验证器任务中会掩盖进步并人为改变智能体排名。能力量表与Epoch评估能力指数排名在Spearman ρ=0.81水平上一致，并可针对领域、职业、行动空间或验证器类型进行专业化。

Conclusion: Messier为智能体能力扩展、基准审计和评估失败细粒度分析提供了基础性、可复用的基础设施。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [265] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"提出-验证"框架的交互式奖励智能体（IRA），通过交互式地获取和执行环境中的证据来评估GUI代理任务完成情况。在GUIRewardBench基准上达到86.9%准确率，并可将评估结果作为强化学习奖励信号，提升GUI代理在OSWorld上的成功率至34.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的GUI任务评估需要访问环境状态（如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等），而不仅仅依赖执行轨迹的截图，现有方法难以准确获取这些证据。

Method: 提出IRA（Interactive Reward Agent），采用"先提出再验证"框架：先根据任务指令提出任务完成条件，再通过调用系统工具、应用程序工具和GUI工具在交互过程中验证这些条件。

Result: 在GUI-RewardBench基准上达到86.9%准确率，优于现有评估基线；应用于GUI代理的强化学习训练后，OSWorld成功率提升至34.0%。

Conclusion: IRA能够提供有效的奖励信号用于训练GUI代理，证明了交互式环境状态验证对GUI任务评估的重要性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [266] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Penelope框架，通过在解码器的一个选定区间内进行局部化潜在推理，利用时间调制GRU动态和循环读出状态进行迭代精炼，配合渐进式CoT到潜在空间的课程学习，在不增加完整解码器执行次数或生成冗长中间推理链的情况下，提升结构推理任务的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型处理复杂结构化推理任务主要依赖增大参数规模或生成链式思维（CoT）中间步骤，前者增加训练和部署成本，后者将推理计算与自回归输出长度绑定，效率受限。

Method: Penelope框架将循环计算局部化到解码器的一个选定区间内：下层解码前缀一次性计算构建问题条件边界记忆，通过时间调制GRU动态和循环读出状态进行迭代精炼，并采用渐进式CoT到潜在空间的课程学习，将可见推理迁移到内部循环路径中。

Result: 在开源结构化推理基准测试中，在验证选择的潜在预算下，Penelope达到了与已有潜在推理模型相当的准确率，同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: 潜在精炼可以局部化到较窄的解码器区间内，减少重复的全解码器执行，无需生成冗长的可见推理轨迹，为解码器-only Transformer模型提供了实用的准确率-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [267] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了dtControl2工具，通过引入ε-允许误差机制，在保证控制器ε-最优性的前提下，构建更小、更易理解的决策树，大幅简化了大规模系统的可解释表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树控制器表示工具（如dtControl2）在处理大规模系统或含大量边界情况的系统时，生成的决策树过于复杂，难以被人类理解，而直接缩小决策树又会导致关键信息的丢失。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的设定下，为dtControl2扩展了"ε"功能，允许用户设定一个容忍误差ε≥0，在保证控制器ε-最优性的前提下，构建更小的决策树，从而提炼控制器的核心本质。

Result: 新工具构建的决策树比现有技术水平小数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过允许可控的精度损失，可以在保持控制器性能保证的前提下，大幅简化决策树的复杂度，实现可调节的解释性提升。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [268] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解器引导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，用于多仓库库存分配中的运筹学（OR）配方选择，通过GRPO训练显著提升了配方案例的选择准确率和实际分配质量，在京东数据上Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，分配准确率较基线提升12.57个百分点，将与事后最优的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库库存分配通常被建模为混合整数规划（MIP）问题，但没有任何单一配方能始终匹配由需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动性等因素导致的异构实例级场景，因此需要进行实例级运筹学配方选择。

Method: 提出求解器引导的LLM框架，构建专家条件化的监督微调（SFT）记录进行模式学习，然后利用MIP求解器对历史实例的评估结果，将求解器评估的分配质量差距转化为边际加权身份偏好优化（IPO）偏好和实例级专家分数元数据，再通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）为采样响应分配奖励。

Result: 在京东多仓库库存分配实例上，GRPO将Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%；分配准确率较基线提升12.57个百分点，超越SFT+IPO选择器和最佳固定OR专家，将与事后最优的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: GRPO能显著提升专家选择准确率和实际分配质量，所提出的求解器引导LLM框架有效解决了多仓库库存分配中配方选择的难题。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [269] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHARM是一个多模态图基础模型，通过分层上下文建模实现零样本迁移，用图上下文替代原始节点，结合多模态信息和图结构，在零样本多模态图任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的多模态图（节点包含文本、图像等多模态信息）普遍存在，但为零样本迁移到新图域收集标签和适配模型成本高昂。现有GNN-based图基础模型需要下游适配，LLM-based方法主要处理单模态图，多模态图上的零样本迁移尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出CHARM模型：1) 用分层图上下文替代孤立原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系；2) 将域特定节点模式映射到共享高层概念；3) 模态感知的图上下文编码器整合多模态信息与图结构，将表征转化为图Token供大语言模型处理。

Result: 实验表明CHARM在零样本多模态图任务上取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 分层上下文建模能有效解耦域特定结构与模态特定特征，实现多模态图上的零样本知识迁移。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


### [270] [Falling Behind Drives Unsafe Development in an Idealised AI Race Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26034)
*Elias Fernández Domingos,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过行为实验和进化模型研究AI竞赛中安全与速度的权衡，发现不安全行为更多由竞争动态（对手行为、落后恐惧）驱动，而非单纯的风险偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI竞赛中竞争压力可能激励开发者牺牲安全性以追求更快发展，但这种"速度vs安全"的张力缺乏实证研究，现有争论多停留在理论层面。

Method: 设计框架化行为实验：双人重复博弈中可在"安全"与"不安全"发展间选择，不安全路径进度更快但积累私人风险（最大风险分10%/60%/90%三组）。同时构建简化进化模型（四种策略：始终安全、始终不安全、条件安全、条件反社会安全）解释实验结果。

Result: 预先注册的风险水平比较和风险偏好假设未被验证；探索性分析发现：对手选择不安全后参与者更可能选不安全、领先时减少不安全行为而落后时增加、首轮选择可预测后续行为。

Conclusion: 不安全开发源于早期行为惯性、对手行为和落后恐惧，而非单纯风险偏好；政策应关注减少竞争压力、促进AI开发合作，而非仅管控个体风险。 evolutionary模型表明条件性不安全行为在竞争动态中可能被自然选择所青睐。

Abstract: Technological races create tension between speed and safety: actors may gain by moving faster than competitors, even when risky development is harmful. This is prominent in debates about artificial intelligence (AI), where competitive pressure is often argued to incentivise riskier, less safety-conscious development. We study this using a framed behavioural experiment based on an idealised AI race, in which paired participants repeatedly chose between Safe and Unsafe development under an uncertain time horizon. Unsafe development gave faster progress and higher immediate payoffs but accumulated private risk up to a treatment-specific maximum of 10\%, 60\%, or 90\%; the race's competitive structure was held constant, and only this maximum risk varied. Neither the pre-registered comparison between risk levels nor the role of elicited risk preferences was supported by the data. Instead, exploratory analyses motivated by the task's repeated structure show that Unsafe behaviour is shaped less by risk preferences than by the evolving strategic state of the race: participants are more likely to choose Unsafe after their opponent does so, being ahead reduces Unsafe play while falling behind increases it, and first-round choices predict later behaviour. To interpret these effects we introduce a reduced evolutionary model with four strategies -- Always Safe, Always Unsafe, Conditionally Safe, and Conditionally Antisocial Safe -- which reproduces the treatment effect and shows how conditional Unsafe behaviour can be favoured by competitive race dynamics. Together, the experiment and model show that unsafe development can emerge from early behavioural momentum, opponent behaviour, and fear of falling behind, rather than from risk preferences alone, suggesting policy should focus on reducing competitive pressure and promoting cooperation in AI development rather than only individual risk.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Multimodal User Authentication Method via Fusion of Keystroke Dynamics and Glove-Based Hand Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24747)
*Issei Hyakuda,Lei Jing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合19维手部运动学与按键动力学的多模态认证框架，使用CNN-LSTM混合架构处理数据手套采集的异质时间序列。在未见的跨域场景（台式机训练，笔记本测试）下，单次600ms认证EER为2.12%，10次事件聚合后EER降至0.00%。消融实验表明，虽然IMU静态表现等同，但压力与IMU融合提供了对抗实际环境威胁的关键容错机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按键动力学生物特征虽成本低廉，但在实际部署中易受环境变化影响，导致识别性能不稳定。现有研究缺乏对跨设备、未知攻击者等真实场景的有效验证，亟需更鲁棒的多模态融合方案。

Method: 1. 使用10个压阻式压力传感器+9轴IMU的数据手套采集19维手部运动学特征；2. 采用CNN-LSTM混合架构融合异构时间序列；3. 设计"未见"评估协议：台式机键盘训练（1目标用户+8已知冒名者），笔记本电脑键盘测试（含未知冒名者）；4. 采用时间平滑窗口对多事件分数聚合。

Result: 单次600ms窗口EER为2.12%；10事件（6秒）聚合后EER为0.00%。消融实验显示IMU单独在静态实验室环境下表现等同，但压力与IMU具有对不同误差因素的互补敏感性。

Conclusion: 融合物理特征（压力+IMU）比单纯时序特征提供更强的生物识别区分能力，在跨设备、未知攻击者等实际场景下建立关键容错机制，有效对抗空间欺骗和环境噪声等真实威胁。

Abstract: Although keystroke dynamics are cost-effective behavioral biometrics, their practical deployment is hindered by susceptibility to environmental variations. To address this, we propose a robust multimodal authentication framework that augments traditional keystroke dynamics using 19-dimensional hand kinematics. Features are captured using a bespoke data glove equipped with 10 piezoresistive pressure sensors and a 9-axis IMU. A hybrid CNN-LSTM architecture effectively fuses these heterogeneous time-series streams. To ensure real-world applicability, we implemented a rigorous "unseen" evaluation protocol: the model was trained on a desktop keyboard using data from 1 target user and 8 "known" impostors, but evaluated on a laptop keyboard (cross-domain) against the target and an "unknown" impostor excluded from training. Averaged over five trials, the multimodal method achieved a mean Equal Error Rate (EER) of 2.12% for individual 600-ms authentication events (Window 1). Crucially, aggregating scores over 10 events (6 seconds) using a temporal smoothing window yielded perfect authentication (0.00% EER). While our ablation study showed IMU kinematics alone achieved equivalent performance in a static laboratory, error analysis confirmed that pressure and IMU sensors, despite strong physical correlation, possess distinct sensitivities to error factors. Fusing these modalities establishes a vital fail-safe against real-world vulnerabilities like spatial spoofing and environmental noise, highlighting that combined physical traits provide much stronger biometric discrimination than timing features alone.

</details>


### [272] [The Mirage of LLM Guardrails: A Case Study in AI-Assisted Medical Note Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24859)
*Davis Yadav,Amulya Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型在医疗场景下对恶意查询的防护能力，发现当前商业LLM的防护机制存在显著漏洞，能够通过AI辅助手段生成视觉上难以辨别的虚假医疗记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医疗领域的快速部署使其防护机制可靠性成为紧迫问题，但目前对这些机制在面对恶意滥用时的鲁棒性缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 开发可复现的医疗记录操纵流水线，利用公开模板和商业LLM生成定制化篡改记录；通过自动化指标与人工标注相结合评估操纵正确性；开展用户研究检验篡改记录的可信度。

Result: 实验发现多个商业LLM模型家族对医疗记录操纵请求的拒绝率极低；最佳篡改结果在视觉上对人类评估者而言与原始文档无法区分。

Conclusion: 现有LLM防护机制在医疗场景下存在严重缺陷，需要改进防护设计、制定AI安全政策，并审慎考虑LLM在医疗领域的部署伦理。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of large language models (LLMs) in healthcare settings makes the reliability of their built-in guardrails against malicious queries a question of urgent practical consequence. Yet the robustness of these mechanisms against deliberate misuse (in the healthcare context) remains poorly understood. In this paper, we investigate this question empirically, using AI-assisted medical note manipulation as a concrete case study. We make four novel contributions. First, we develop a reproducible manipulation pipeline that takes publicly available seed medical note templates and use commercial LLMs to produce customized manipulated notes by substituting patient names, provider identities, dates, and medical conditions across multiple model families, input formats, and prompt phrasings. Second, we conduct a systematic empirical evaluation of LLM guardrail robustness for medical note manipulation. Our experimental results reveal substantial weaknesses and inconsistencies in contemporary commercial LLM guardrails, including low refusal rates for several model families. Third, we utilize a combination of automated metrics and human annotation-based metrics to assess the correctness of requested manipulations. Fourth, we conduct a user-study to assess the believability of manipulated medical notes, finding that the best manipulations are visually indistinguishable from original documents to human raters. Finally, we discuss implications for responsible guardrail design in LLMs, AI safety policies, and the broader ethics of deploying LLMs in healthcare settings.

</details>


### [273] [The Missing Layer: Specification Infrastructure for AI Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24866)
*Satyam Kumar,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出AI安全领域存在协调鸿沟而非研究鸿沟，提出5x6分类矩阵（五技术层×六关注点），重点强调第二层（规约层）缺乏成熟工程学科的四项标志，并提出六项设计原则与CARMA原型系统加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全各子领域（可解释性、形式化方法、安全工程、评估方法、强化学习安全）各自产出丰富成果，但无法组合成可部署的监督系统。每个团队都在重复构建审计模式、策略语言、监控栈和升级路径，造成巨大的重复劳动和协调困难。

Method: 提出两轴分类法：五技术层（可读性、规约、中介、评估、升级）×六关注点（对齐、鲁棒性、对抗防御、安全、治理、问责）。聚焦第二层（规约层），提出六项设计原则（可诱导性、可组合性、对抗感知、可追溯性、可治理性），并通过CARMA原型系统在自主ETL代理场景中验证。

Result: 识别出规约层缺乏四项成熟工程学科标志（共享词汇、设计原则、可组合标准、治理实践），并用CARMA证明单一规约可同时驱动执行、评估和升级，所有决策可追溯到版本化规约。

Conclusion: 现有系统（Cedar、Constitutional AI、Open Policy Agent）只解决了规约层的碎片，需要将它们视为共享层的组成部分。CARMA作为概念验证，展示了独立团队可以基于共同原则构建可组合的缺失模块。

Abstract: AI safety has a missing layer. Interpretability, formal methods, security engineering, evaluation methodology, and reinforcement-learning safety each produce substantial work, but the resulting artifacts do not compose into deployable oversight: every team fielding an agentic system builds its own audit schema, policy dialect, monitoring stack, and escalation path, mostly reinventions of patterns understood elsewhere. We diagnose this as a coordination gap, not a research gap, and propose a two-axis taxonomy: five technical layers (Legibility, Specification, Mediation, Evaluation, Escalation) crossed with six concerns spanning alignment, robustness, adversarial defense, security, governance, and accountability, populating the resulting 5x6 matrix with existing work. Layer 2 (Specification), where humans translate intent into machine-checkable artifacts, is the connective tissue every layer depends on, yet it lacks four marks of a mature engineering discipline: shared vocabulary, design principles, composability standards, and governance practices. We propose six design principles for Layer 2, from elicitability and composability to adversary-awareness, traceability, and governability, made concrete through worked examples and a reference architecture turning specifications into runtime enforcement, evaluation, and escalation. Existing systems such as Cedar, Constitutional AI, and Open Policy Agent each address a fragment of Layer 2 well and the matrix poorly; treating them as fragments of one shared layer makes composition tractable. As evidence, we introduce CARMA, a Layer 2 prototype for autonomous ETL agents in which one specification drives enforcement, evaluation, and escalation, with every decision traceable to a versioned specification, naming what AI oversight is missing and giving independent teams principles to build the missing pieces so they compose.

</details>


### [274] [Trusting-Trust Attack against an Entire Linux Distribution through Binary Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24888)
*Julien Malka,Aman Sharma,Martin Monperrus,Stefano Zacchiroli,Théo Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明Ken Thompson的信任信任攻击不仅限于编译器，通过篡改GNU strip这一普通构建工具，同样可以在NixOS Linux发行版中实现自我传播的后门攻击，影响几乎所有二进制文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"信任信任攻击仅适用于编译器"的传统认知，证明普通构建工具同样易受此类攻击，揭示软件供应链安全的更广泛威胁。

Method: 以GNU strip为目标（该工具不检查也不生成源代码），仅通过操作ELF文件，在NixOS的二进制种子中植入篡改的strip，使其在后代版本中自我传播，最终渗透到标准环境中。

Result: 在真实nixpkgs版本上，攻击成功构建了完整的图形安装程序，几乎感染了所有二进制文件，使被篡改的软件包能够执行任意恶意行为。

Conclusion: 信任信任攻击的威胁范围远超编译器，普通构建工具同样可能被利用来实施自我传播的供应链后门攻击。

Abstract: Ken Thompson's trusting-trust attack, in which a compromised compiler backdoors the programs it builds and reproduces the backdoor in subsequent rebuilds of itself, is widely regarded as a threat specific to compilers. We show that it is not. We construct a complete trusting-trust attack around GNU strip, an ordinary build utility that neither inspects nor generates source code, using only manipulations of finished ELF files. In the bootstrap of the NixOS Linux distribution, a single tampered strip in the binary seed implants a payload that propagates from one generation of strip to the next and survives into the final standard environment after the seed leaves the dependency closure. On a real nixpkgs revision, the attack builds a complete graphical installer without failures and backdoors almost every one of its binaries, enabling arbitrary malicious behavior of the subverted packages.

</details>


### [275] [Early Detection of Distributed Backdoors in Multi-Agent LLM Systems: A Characterization Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24893)
*Diego Fernandez Arias,Dev Prashant Mistry,Ren Wang,Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统可遭受分布式隐写攻击：恶意工具将加密负载碎片分散于多个智能体的观察中，运行结束后外部步骤重组并执行。逐步骤安全检查因孤立判断而失效。研究发现，在负载组装前，前缀检测器可在中位5步前以99.3%检出率预警，但检测效果部分依赖负载的熵和长度等表层特征；移除这些特征后，检测延迟且跨域迁移性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐步骤安全检测无法识别跨智能体分发的分布式恶意负载，攻击者利用"无单一智能体持有完整负载"的特性规避检测，亟需研究此类攻击的可检测性及检测时限。

Method: 在分层多智能体系统上构建攻击实例，跨5个语言模型和2个任务域运行良性/攻击场景，记录碎片注入与负载组装时间。使用前缀检测器、零样本检测器、行为训练检测器及微调模型进行检测对比，并分析去除熵和长度特征后的检测性能变化。

Result: 前缀检测器在负载组装前以中位5步剩余步骤检出99.3%成功攻击，误报率10.3%。但零样本和行为检测器几乎无预警能力；去除熵和长度特征后，检测延迟且跨域迁移性恶化，微调模型可部分恢复损失。

Conclusion: 分布式攻击可在早期被检测，但预警依赖于可移除的表层特征（负载长度和熵），而非攻击的分布式结构本身。去除这些特征后检测性能显著下降，提示需要更鲁棒的检测方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can be attacked by a payload that no single agent ever holds in full: a poisoned tool hides encrypted fragments in its observations, spreads them across several agents, and an external step reassembles and executes them after the run. Per-step safety checks that judge each action in isolation may fail to recognize the complete distributed payload. We investigate how early such an attack can be detected while the run is still unfolding, and how robustly it can be caught once its most obvious cues are stripped away. We build a working instance on a hierarchical multi-agent system, run it under benign and attacked conditions across five language models and two task domains, and record when each fragment is injected and when the payload is assembled and executed. Detection is a race against assembly. Before the first fragment is injected, attacked and benign runs are indistinguishable; once injection begins, a prefix detector flags $99.3\%$ of successful attacks with a median of five steps remaining and a $10.3\%$ safe-run false-positive rate. Because assembly occurs only after the run, these alarms arrive in time to abort nearly every successful attack. We then measure how much of that warning rests on removable surface cues of the attack rather than on its distributed structure. Generic zero-shot and behavior-trained detectors provide almost no warning at all; the detectors that do work lean in part on removable surface cues, chiefly the ciphertext's length and entropy, and once the entropy cue is removed from the payload and the length features from the detector, detection arrives later and transfers poorly across domains, though a fine-tuned model recovers some of the loss.

</details>


### [276] [Latent Stability Analysis of Malware Representations Under Feature-Space Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24896)
*Bamidele Ajayi,Ken McGarry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于恶意软件扰动评估的潜在稳定性分析管道，在EMBER特征空间中比较了多种表示方法，并定义了LED等新指标来测量扰动下的潜在行为。全EMBER特征在干净分类上表现最佳（ROC AUC 0.9962），而VAE+Mandelbrot+PINNFlow组合虽未超越基线，但在扰动诊断上提供了额外价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态恶意软件检测器主要依赖准确率、F1、ROC AUC等干净样本指标，这些指标无法揭示特征向量被扰动时潜在表示的行为变化、样本向不确定决策区域移动的程度，以及压缩表示是否保留了与安全相关的结构信息。

Method: 构建潜在稳定性分析管道，比较全EMBER特征、PCA压缩、贝塔/去噪变分自编码器表示、Mandelbrot逃逸时间描述符以及PINN样式潜在流模块；定义Latent Escape Divergence (LED)指标测量扰动下逃逸时间轮廓的变化，并使用PINNFlow派生的残差、速度、风险、梯度偏移等指标表征潜在运动。

Result: 全EMBER特征干净分类性能最强（ROC AUC 0.9962, F1 0.9713），PCA-64为最佳压缩基线（ROC AUC 0.9846, F1 0.9347）。VAE+Mandelbrot+PINNFlow组合在干净分类上未超越基线，但在受控特征空间扰动探测下提供额外诊断价值。

Conclusion: 传统干净样本指标不足以评估恶意软件检测器的鲁棒性，所提出的潜在稳定性分析管道和LED等指标能够揭示扰动下表示行为的关键信息，为理解模型在对抗性环境中的表现提供补充视角。

Abstract: Static malware detectors are commonly evaluated using clean-sample metrics such as accuracy, F1, ROC AUC, and PR AUC. However, these metrics provide limited insight into how learned malware representations behave when feature vectors are perturbed, how close samples move toward uncertain decision regions, or whether compressed representations preserve security-relevant structure. This paper presents a latent-stability analysis pipeline for malware perturbation assessment in EMBER feature space. The pipeline compares full EMBER features, PCA-based compression, beta/denoising variational autoencoder representations, Mandelbrot-inspired escape-time descriptors, and a PINN-style latent-flow module. We define Latent Escape Divergence (LED) to measure changes in escape-time profiles under perturbation, and use PINNFlow-derived residual, velocity, risk, and gradient-shift metrics to characterize latent movement. Experiments are conducted on EMBER static PE feature vectors using 180,000 training samples, 180,000 test samples, and 240,000 holdout samples. Full EMBER features achieve the strongest clean classification performance with ROC AUC of 0.9962 and F1 of 0.9713, while PCA-64 is the strongest compressed baseline with ROC AUC of 0.9846 and F1 of 0.9347. The proposed VAE+Mandelbrot+PINNFlow representation does not outperform these baselines for clean classification, but it provides additional diagnostic value under controlled feature-space perturbation probes.

</details>


### [277] [TYPO: Instruction-Dense Visual Jailbreaks against Commercial Closed-Source Image-Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24897)
*Meng Xie,Li Zeng,Hangtao Zhang,Xianlong Wang,Ziqi Zhou,Pengpeng Qiao,Zhetao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现图像生成模型存在一种新的安全漏洞：即使模型拒绝直接输出有害文本，但可通过生成包含可读有害文字的图像绕过安全对齐。作者提出TYPO框架，通过双通道（文本+视觉）对抗提示生成，自动创建将有害意图转化为高可读性排版文字的提示，在四个商业模型上以平均50.2%的攻击成功率超越九种已有方法，每次查询成本仅0.04美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型的安全对齐主要针对文本输出和视觉内容，但未考虑文字嵌入图像这一跨模态安全漏洞——模型可能拒绝直接输出有害文本，却允许将同样内容以图像中可读文字的形式生成。作者旨在揭示并利用这一安全鸿沟。

Method: 提出TYPO框架，将提示生成分解为文本通道（重构有害意图）和视觉通道（指定呈现形式）的双通道策略空间，通过自适应组合搜索优化策略组合，在黑盒条件下自动生成对抗性排版提示。

Result: 在GPT-Image-2、Nano Banana Pro、Qwen-Image-2、Seedream 5.0 Lite四个商业模型上，TYPO平均攻击成功率（ASR）比九种代表性越狱攻击高出50.2%，每次查询成本仅0.04美元。

Conclusion: 证明了图像生成模型的安全对齐在跨模态场景下存在严重漏洞，即文本对齐保护不会可靠地迁移到图像中嵌入的文字。TYPO框架高效且低成本地展示了这一威胁，提醒研究者和开发者需要针对跨模态安全对齐进行加固。

Abstract: Recent commercial image-generation models can generate high-quality images with readable text (e.g., posters, infographics, and manuals), attracting considerable attention. Yet we first show that this same capability also introduces a previously unreported safety vulnerability: these systems may refuse to generate harmful text directly, yet permit the same content when rendered as text within generated images, i.e., safety alignment does not reliably transfer from textual outputs to text embedded in images. In this paper, unlike existing visual jailbreaks against image-generation models, which primarily induce models to generate harmful visual objects or scenes, we introduce the concept of instruction-dense visual jailbreaks, in which image-generation models produce detailed, readable, and actionable harmful instructions within images. Such outputs can amplify harm because the rendered instructions can be readily read and widely spread. To instantiate this threat, we propose TYPO, a black-box framework that exploits this safety gap by automatically generating adversarial TYPOgraphy prompts, which covertly steer image-generation models to express harmful intent as highly legible, typographically structured text. Specifically, TYPO decomposes prompt generation into two channels: a textual channel for reframing the target intent, and a visual channel for specifying its presentation form. We formulate these two channels as a dual-channel textual-visual strategy space and optimize candidate strategy combinations through an adaptive combinatorial search. Extensive experiments across four commercial models (i.e., GPT-Image-2, Nano Banana Pro, Qwen-Image-2, and Seedream 5.0 Lite) show that TYPO substantially outperforms nine representative jailbreak attacks by 50.2% in ASR on average, while incurring an average query cost of only $0.04.

</details>


### [278] [ALIBI: Adaptive Agentic Attacks on LLM-Based Vulnerability Detectors via Adversarial Code Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24964)
*Zixuan Wu,Cristina Nita-Rotaru*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ALIBI框架，通过自动生成对抗性注释来欺骗基于LLM的漏洞检测器，在125个真实空指针解引用漏洞上攻击成功率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在安全敏感任务（如漏洞检测）中依赖源代码中的自然语言上下文，这暴露了未被充分探索的攻击面：对抗性注释可在不改变程序行为的情况下影响检测器推理。

Method: 提出ALIBI，一种自动化自适应黑盒攻击框架，利用检测器推理和反馈生成并迭代优化对抗性注释。将真实漏洞修复提交转化为编码任务进行评估。

Result: 四种检测器攻击成功率均超90%，其中一个系统达100%。对抗性注释可引导检测器推理或伪造外部工具结果，迭代优化进一步提高成功率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM漏洞检测器存在根本性攻击面，提示级防御对自适应攻击效果有限，而架构隔离和注释预处理可显著提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed for security-sensitive tasks such as vulnerability detection and code review. Their reliance on natural-language context embedded in source code exposes a previously underexplored attack surface: adversarial comments that can influence a detector's reasoning without changing program behavior. We study LLM-based vulnerability detectors against a new adversary: a coding agent that implements new functionality, deliberately introduces vulnerabilities, and strategically inserts adversarial source-code comments to evade detection.
  We present ALIBI, an automated adaptive black-box attack framework that generates and iteratively refines adversarial comments using detector reasoning and feedback. We transform real-world vulnerability-fixing commits into coding tasks and evaluate four representative LLM-based vulnerability detectors, ranging from specialized open-weight reasoning models to frontier multi-agent systems. All evaluated detectors are highly vulnerable: attack success rates exceed 90% across 125 real-world null-pointer dereference vulnerabilities, reaching 100% on one system. The framework also generalizes beyond this vulnerability class. Adversarial comments steering detector reasoning or fabricating external tool results prove most effective, while iterative refinement based on detector feedback further increases attack success. Finally, prompt-level defenses provide limited robustness against adaptive attacks, whereas architectural isolation and pre-detector comment sanitization substantially improve resilience. Our findings expose a fundamental attack surface in current LLM-based vulnerability detectors and motivate security-aware designs that carefully calibrate trust between natural-language context and program evidence.

</details>


### [279] [ZIMPAF & RedPhuzz: High-fidelity Web Application Fuzzing via Branch, Language Construct, and Function Call Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25012)
*Tennov Simanjuntak,Christoph Csallner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ZIMPAF运行时解释器插桩和RedPhuzz模糊测试器，通过多粒度插桩、新型变异策略和多阶段漏洞检测，解决了现有模糊测试器效率低、缺乏执行环境感知和Web领域知识有限的问题。相比Phuzz，RedPhuzz检测到所有漏洞（Phuzz漏检16个），速度快73%，吞吐量提升2.1-41.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试器存在三个关键局限：低效的插桩机制、缺乏对执行环境的感知、以及有限的Web领域知识，导致漏检和效率低下。

Method: 提出ZIMPAF多粒度运行时解释器插桩，提供分支覆盖率、错误和异常日志、函数和语言结构监控、用户输入分支指令识别；通过反向常量探针推断不可漏洞函数并跳过；RedPhuzz利用这些信息进行高度目标化的函数级和输入级模糊测试，引入三种新型变异策略：感知消毒、分支输入感知、数据类型感知。

Result: 在6个基准Web应用的86个测试用例中，RedPhuzz检测到所有漏洞，Phuzz漏检16个；RedPhuzz比Phuzz快73%；ZIMPAF吞吐量比PCOV和UOPZ高2.1-41.22倍。

Conclusion: ZIMPAF和RedPhuzz通过更高效、更具目标性的插桩和模糊测试方法，显著提升了Web应用漏洞检测的全面性和效率。

Abstract: We present ZIMPAF, runtime interpreter instrumentation, and RedPhuzz, a fuzzer, to address key limitations of state-of-the-art fuzzers: inefficient instrumentation, the lack of knowledge of the execution environment, and limited web domain knowledge. ZIMPAF implements a novel multi-granular runtime interpreter instrumentation that provides branch coverage, robust error and exception logging, function and language construct monitoring, and identification of user-supplied inputs used in branch instructions. The instrumentation is capable of identifying potentially vulnerable functions whose parameters are tainted, marking them as high-valued fuzzing targets, without performing taint analysis. It also employs a novel backward constant probe to infer potentially vulnerable functions whose parameters originate from constants, indicating their invulnerability and allowing them to be skipped. This information is utilized by RedPhuzz to perform highly-targeted function-and input-level fuzzing that goes beyond simple error-based fuzzing, but also detects silent vulnerabilities via multi-stage vulnerability detection. We also introduce three novel mutation strategies to achieve highly targeted and effective fuzzing: sanitization-aware, input-in-branch-aware, and data type-aware mutation. We evaluate RedPhuzz's performance with its predecessor (Phuzz) with 86 test cases across six benchmark web applications. RedPhuzz detects all vulnerabilities, while Phuzz fails to detect 16. RedPhuzz is 73% faster than Phuzz despite performing more tasks. ZIMPAF is faster than Phuzz's instrumentation (PCOV and UOPZ), while writing significantly more data. ZIMPAF achieves 2.1 to 41.22 times higher throughput than PCOV and UOPZ across five sampled benchmarks, and 0.87 times for one.

</details>


### [280] [An Attack on High Rate McEliece Cryptosystems Using Generalized Reed Solomon Codes with Weight $2$ Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25027)
*Julia Lieb,Abhinaba Mazumder,Michael Schaller*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出基于GRS码的McEliece类密码系统中，用权重大于1的矩阵M替换置换矩阵的方案存在安全隐患。针对高码率场景，提出了区分攻击方法，并建立了从区分攻击到密钥恢复攻击的框架，成功攻击了行/列权重为2的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McEliece密码系统使用GRS码存在不安全性，后续方案通过增大矩阵M的行列权重来增强安全性（如权重为2或更高），但此前攻击仅覆盖权重在1到1+R之间的情形，权重为2及以上的安全性尚未被充分验证。

Method: 提出了高码率场景下对公钥码的区分器（如立方码区分器），并构建了将区分攻击转化为密钥恢复攻击的通用框架。

Result: 成功区分了高码率下权重为2的M矩阵方案中的公钥码，并在此基础上实现了密钥恢复攻击。

Conclusion: 行列权重为2的M矩阵在高码率场景下不足以保障McEliece类密码系统的安全性，相关方案存在被攻破的风险。

Abstract: Due to the insecurity of McEliece cryptosystems instantiated with Generalized Reed-Solomon codes, there have been several proposals of McEliece type systems that replace the permutation matrix by a matrix $M$ with larger row and column weight. In many of them, the secret key is still a GRS code. There have been successful attacks on some of those schemes with row and column weight between $1$ and $1 + R$, where $R$ is the rate of the code. The case of weight two and larger has been left open in these works. Subsequently, several authors proposed schemes with weight exactly two and with even higher weight. We provide distinguishers for the public codes appearing in these cryptosystems in the high rate regime. In addition, we give a framework to turn a good enough distinguisher into a key-recovery attack. In the case where the matrix $M$ has row and column weight $2$, we can successfully attack the scheme in the high rate regime using a cube code distinguisher.

</details>


### [281] [MOSAIC-FL, a micro-service based privacy-preserving framework with application to genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25107)
*Paul Largillier,Karl Paygambar,Cédric Gouy-Pailler,Vincent Meyer,Mallek Mziou,Oana Stan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个模块化、灵活的微服务联邦学习框架，集成gRPC通信、有限状态机、容错安全聚合协议（基于Threshold CKKS同态加密），通过噪声淹没和密钥更新确保IND-CPA-D安全，并在图像识别和基因组分类任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中敏感数据（如医疗和基因组数据）的安全与隐私保护是核心需求，现有框架在安全性、灵活性和通信效率方面仍有不足。

Method: 采用微服务架构，集成gRPC高效通信层和有限状态机进行组件同步与威胁检测；使用基于Threshold CKKS同态加密的容错安全聚合协议，实现t-out-of-N门限解密；通过噪声淹没和每轮更新集体密钥材料来抵御密钥恢复攻击。

Result: 在EMNIST图像识别和TCGA乳腺癌亚型基因组分类等任务上，评估了不同门限值和模型规模下的系统性能，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在保障安全隐私（IND-CPA-D安全）的同时，具备模块化、灵活性和低通信开销，适用于医疗和基因组等敏感领域的联邦学习。

Abstract: Security and privacy are primordial requirements for Federated Learning (FL), especially in fields such as healthcare and genomics where sensitive information has to be analyzed. Our FL framework is designed to address these challenges while proposing a modular, flexible and micro-service architecture. More precisely, it integrates an efficient gRPC communication layer and a Finite State Machine to ensure robust component synchronization and threat detection, while relying on a fault-tolerant secure aggregation protocol using a Threshold variant of the CKKS homomorphic cryptosystem. This allows blind model aggregation by an orchestration server, requiring a minimum of $t$-out-of-$N$ active clients for decryption while minimizing communication overhead thanks to both cryptographic and network protocols. We ensure IND-CPA-D security through noise flooding and mitigate the recent key-recovery attack on synchronized decryptors by renewing the collective key material at every round. We demonstrate the framework's effectiveness through diverse use cases, ranging from standard image recognition (EMNIST) to complex genomic classification including breast cancer subtyping on TCGA, evaluating system performance across different threshold values and model scales.

</details>


### [282] [How to Watermark the RLWE Homomorphic Ciphertexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25222)
*Yufei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对RLWE同态加密方案缺乏水印保护的问题，提出了ARWMark（基于噪声分层，抗加法操作）和MRWMark（基于线性方程根，抗加法和乘法操作，支持零比特水印）两种水印技术，在不影响原加密安全性的前提下实现了密文版权保护与监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLWE同态加密方案多关注计算可行性与效率，忽视了密文版权保护、来源认证和计算过程中的监管等实际需求。

Method: 利用RLWE多项式代数结构，将微小噪声作为水印嵌入密文多项式；提出ARWMark（噪声分层策略，抗同态加法）和MRWMark（线性方程根构造，抗同态加法和乘法，支持零比特水印）两种方案。

Result: 理论证明方案不破坏HE原有安全性，保证水印正确性与鲁棒性；实验验证了两种方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的水印方案填补了RLWE同态加密在版权保护和监管方面的空白，在保持安全性的同时实现了鲁棒的水印嵌入与提取。

Abstract: In recent years, homomorphic encryption (HE) schemes based on the Ring Learning with Errors (RLWE) problem have rapidly developed and been widely applied to secure computation tasks, including privacy-preserving deep learning inference, privacy-preserving database queries, and related applications. However, most existing HE schemes focus primarily on the feasibility and efficiency of homomorphic computation, often neglecting practical requirements such as copyright protection of ciphertexts, source authentication, and supervision during computation. To address these issues, we propose a watermarking technique for RLWE-based HE ciphertexts. The algebraic structure of RLWE polynomials allows us to embed small noise as watermarking information into the ciphertext polynomials without affecting the plaintext values. However, HE ciphertexts typically undergo multiple homomorphic operations, which can distort or even remove the embedded watermark information. To address this challenge, we propose two practical solutions. The first, ARWMark, is a watermarking scheme based on noise stratification and is robust to homomorphic additive operations. The second scheme, MRWMark, is constructed using the roots of a linear equation and is resilient to both homomorphic additive and multiplicative operations, while supporting zero-bit watermarking. We provide a detailed theoretical analysis, proving that our schemes do not compromise the original security of HE, while ensuring the correctness and robustness of the proposed watermarking techniques. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate the effectiveness of both watermarking schemes.

</details>


### [283] [SecDrift: Measuring Sector-Conditioned Security Drift in AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25225)
*Narayanaswami Natraj Bharadwaj,Dhivya Chandramouleeswaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecDrift基准测试发现，LLM生成代码的漏洞率在不同行业提示条件下并无显著差异（11.4% vs 14.0%，p=0.24），模型选择（11.6%-16.1%）而非提示领域上下文才是影响安全性的更可靠因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于关键基础设施的代码生成，但领域特定提示对安全性的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 构建SecDrift基准，对7个LLM（其中6个可产出可分析代码）在8个CISA关键基础设施部门和9个CWE类别上进行5,355次评估，采用5维变换和匹配基线条件，通过Fisher精确检验、Cohen's h和混合效应逻辑回归分析。

Result: 行业提示看起来更安全（14.0% vs 11.4%，-2.7pp），但差异不显著（p=0.24, h=-0.08），且是CWE-502和CWE-22两类漏洞的组成伪像；去除后趋势反转。8个部门均未显示可区分漂移（|h|<0.15）；非CISA部门的安慰剂实验复制了相近结果（10.5% vs 11.4%, p=0.63）。模型选择效果显著且一致（11.6%-16.1%）。

Conclusion: 模型选择比提示框架更可靠的安全杠杆；领域特定提示对代码安全性的影响可忽略不计，行业上下文造成的安全漂移主要是统计伪像。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for code generation in critical infrastructure, yet the security effect of domain-specific prompting is understudied. We present SecDrift, a benchmark measuring sector-conditioned security drift: the change in static-analysis vulnerability rates when prompts are conditioned on industry contexts versus neutral baselines. We evaluate 7 LLMs (6 producing analyzable code) across 8 CISA critical infrastructure sectors and 9 CWE categories with 5 replicates (5,355 evaluations), using a 5-dimension transformation with a matched-baseline condition that holds the task fixed while substituting only domain terminology. Industry prompts naively appear more secure (14.0% vs. 11.4%, -2.7pp), but the gap is not statistically significant (Fisher's exact p = 0.24, Cohen's h = -0.08) and is a composition artifact of two CWE categories: excluding CWE-502 and CWE-22 eliminates and slightly reverses it (+0.4pp, p = 1.00). A mixed-effects logistic regression confirms sector identity is not a moderator and localizes the only detectable condition effect to those two vulnerability types. 0 of 8 sectors show drift distinguishable from baseline, corrected or uncorrected (|h| < 0.15). A placebo on two non-CISA sectors (e-commerce, online education) reproduces the CISA industry rate almost exactly (10.5% vs. 11.4%, p = 0.63): the small pooled pattern reflects generic industry-framing specificity, not critical-infrastructure identity. In contrast, model selection has a large and consistent effect: among full-output models vulnerability rates range from 11.6% to 16.1%, and these differences persist across conditions. Model choice, not prompt framing, is the more reliable security lever. We release the framework, prompts, generated code, findings, human-validation verdicts, and analysis scripts.

</details>


### [284] [Decision-Level Hijacking: Injecting Cognitive Bias into Large Language Models via Bit-Flip Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25227)
*Yu Yan,Jiahao Chen,Siqi Lu,Yongjuan Wang,Ziming Zhao,Zhaoxuan Li,Tianyu Du,Qingjun Yuan,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了比特翻转攻击（BFA）可作为一种新型攻击向量，通过在部署后翻转极少量权重比特，实现对LLM认知立场的隐蔽、低成本和持久操控，从而引发决策级劫持。作者提出CogBias框架，通过可微情感评估器、多目标损失和BitScout关键位定位技术，在超稀疏比特预算下实现定向认知干预。实验表明，翻转少量比特即可显著影响模型在目标话题上的立场，而对非目标任务影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在战略决策等高危场景中广泛应用，但开源模型共享生态与LLM决策应用的深度融合带来了新风险：攻击者可通过操控模型的认知立场，间接影响下游决策者的判断。现有攻击无法在不触发违禁内容或降低模型功能的前提下实现定向认知操控，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: CogBias框架包含三个核心组件：1）可微情感评估器，将主观偏好转化为优化信号；2）多目标损失函数，联合约束多个维度；3）BitScout算法，定位关键比特位。通过比特翻转攻击，在部署后仅翻转极少量权重比特（无需实时交互或控制训练过程），实现对LLM认知立场的定向操控。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-14B上，以及商业推荐和有争议事实话题场景中，仅翻转少量比特即可稳定诱导模型在目标话题上产生显著立场偏移，同时对非目标任务和整体输出分布的影响有限。

Conclusion: 对底层权重数据的微小扰动足以破坏LLM的高层价值对齐。这一发现揭示了LLM在实际部署中面临的新型安全威胁，决策级劫持需引起高度重视。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied in high-stakes decision-making scenarios such as corporate strategy, and users are increasingly relying on their outputs.
